# B7.6 · Exhaustive pair diff — all 29 Settings surfaces

Generated for the B7.6 revisit, per `REDO_SETTINGS_FIDELITY.md` §3. One row per deviation:
**element · prototype · build · verdict**. Verdicts: `fix` (default — the prototype is truth), `adopt-build-with-reason`
(honesty gate / canon rule the prototype violates / real prototype defect — the category is named in every row),
`spec-amend` (a `SURFACE_SPEC_*.md` line contradicts the prototype; the line is amended in this PR), `match` (no deviation — recorded so the inventory is complete).

## Settings hub

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-hub-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-hub-1440x900.png`

MATCHES — verified, not deviations, so not rowed: all six icon glyphs (◉ ✉ ◍ ⛨ ◆ ⇄) and all six tint triples are byte-identical, sampled from the frames — core/credits #EAF5EE+#CFE8D8+#146B33, senders #E2F3F6+#BFE3EB+#0E7D93, team #F0EDF9+#DCD5EF+#5B4A8A, guard #F7EFDA+#EAD9A8+#8A6D1A, integrations #EAF3F5+#CFE4E9+#356170; icon tile 34×34 radius 12 at identical x in both; the affordance is a '→' (13px, #8B968F) not a chevron, at the same pixel bbox (604,174)-(612,177) in both; title 14.5px/800/-.022em; sub 12.5px/#5A6660/1.55/margin-top 11; grid `repeat(auto-fit,minmax(240px,1fr))` gap 12 with padding 26px 40px 40px (measured 12px row gap in both); card radius 20 in both; container is an inline grid in the canvas in both — no modal, no drawer, no pinned footer, no step counter, no button inside any card; Guardrails and Credits sub-lines are identical strings, and the Credits card is the only one showing a number ('2,340 left.') in both; the Ada bar, page eyebrow and the title's gradient ink match.

COULD NOT BE JUDGED FROM THE FRAME — needs a live check:
· Integrations pill. Prototype shows '3 connected' (live tone); the build frame shows no pill. The build renders it only when `connectedIntegrations > 0`, and null (a failed `/integrations` read) and 0 both produce the same bare card. I cannot tell from the frame which happened. If the fixture genuinely has nothing connected the absence is defensible; if the read returned null it is silently swallowing an error, and either way the card has no absent-state wording the way Senders' 'Warm-up not started' does.
· Consequence of the two missing pills: the build's row-2 cards measure 126px tall against the prototype's 139px (row 1 is 161 vs 159, i.e. matching). The 13px shortfall is entirely the Integrations pill's box, so it should resolve with the pill and is not a separate spacing defect.
· Fixture divergence is expected and I did not row it: the build ran against a Demo/dental workspace (8 facts, 8 gaps, 3 members, 2 senders at 20% warm-up) vs the prototype's Bright Smile fixture (14 facts, 2 gaps, 2 people, 82%). Only composition, order and wording were judged, never the numbers themselves.

SHELL CHROME VISIBLE IN THE PAIR BUT OUTSIDE THIS SURFACE — flagged, not rowed, since they belong to the rail/canvas and would recur in every B7.5 frame:
· The build's rail header carries a '«' collapse button the prototype has no counterpart for, and its width cost truncates the workspace name to 'Dem…' and the eyebrow to 'WORKSP…' where the prototype renders 'Bright Smile' / 'WORKSPACE · 1 OF 3' in full. The truncation looks like a real defect rather than a data artefact.
· The build paints a white wedge tail on the canvas's right edge at y≈475 (`.cvb-canvas-tail`, pointing at the active dock tile). The prototype has nothing there.
· Rail section order is swapped: the prototype runs CAMPAIGNS → ALWAYS ON/INBOUND → ✦ ADA SUGGESTS → Business core → CREDITS; the build runs CAMPAIGNS → ✦ ADA SUGGESTS → ALWAYS ON/INBOUND → Business core → CREDITS.

SETTLED ITEMS I DELIBERATELY DID NOT RAISE, per the brief: typed guardrail wells, the credits hero's USED/ADDED gating, the 'nothing has drawn down credits' line, the forest Top up button, 'Member' over 'Agent', and gaps attributed to campaigns.

Sampled in the frame: card interior ramps #FFFFFF (y141) → #F7FAF8 (y292), border row reads #E4E6E5, and a shadow ramp #F0F0F0→#FAFAFA runs for ~8px below the border. apps/web/components/bold/settings/BoldSettingsHub.tsx:140. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md line 35 is the sentence that settles it and it agrees with the prototype — 'Panel gradients #FFFFFF → #F7FAF8 on raised cards; flat #FCFCFC on quiet cards.' | **Senders sub-line — the counted noun** | "Two email domains and one number." | **Senders sub-line — the zero** | "…and one number." — a stated quantity. | "…and 0 numbers." The hub is the one place in this family that renders the bare zero. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>The build's own doctrine (settings-data.ts header, and 'Warm-up not started' on this very card) is to word an absence rather than draw it; the hub card is inconsistent with the page inside it.</sub> |
| **Team and roles sub-line — role list and trailing clause** | "Two people plus Ada. Owner, admin, viewer." Three role words for two humans plus Ada — the third word covers Ada's own role. The Senders item page in the same run carries an amber 'Needs a look' header pill and two rows reading 'Unchecked' — so the hub says nothing while the page inside it says something needs attention. `dnsPosture()` already has the 'unchecked' vocabulary ('Not checked yet') the hub does not use. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Breaks the family's own stated invariant (settings-data.ts header: the hub's counts and the page inside it can never disagree). ## Business core → What she knows

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-core-facts-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-core-facts-1440x900.png`

MATCHES (checked, not deviations): no leading icon on fact rows in either — the prototype gives icons only to the `sources` (plum ◍/◫ on #F0EDF9/#DCD5EF/#5B4A8A), `email`/`num` and `fields` tabs, never to `facts`, and the build matches. Row name typography identical (13.5px / 700 / -.016em / lh 1.4). Row padding identical (15px 4px, gap 14). Chip geometry and mint tone identical (10px/700, radius 999, padding 3px 9px, #146B33 on #EAF5EE / #CFE8D8). Sub-line ORDER is the same in both — the fact's value and nothing else, no prefix, no trailing meta. Tab row identical (padding 22px 40px 0, gap 22, 14px, active 800/#101613 with a 2px #146B33 underline, inactive 500/#8B968F). Status pill identical (10.5px/700, radius 999, padding 6px 12px, margin-top 9; warn = #8A6D1A on #F7EFDA / #EAD9A8, which the build reaches via tone \"capped\"). Add row identical in position (inside the list block, after the rows, before the ✦ note) and in style (32px dashed #CFD6D1 tile, radius 11, faint \"+\" 15px, label 13px/700 in cyan #0E7D93) and label (\"Add something she should know\"). ✦ Ada card identical (mint #EAF5EE, 1px #CFE8D8, radius 20, padding 18px 20px, margin-top 28, forest ✦ 13px, body 13px #1D5B34 lh 1.6, forest button 12.5px/800 radius 12 padding 11px 16px margin-top 14). Meta-strip container identical (padding 12px 40px, #FCFCFC, #F1F2F1 top and bottom borders). No price/rate/date deviation on this surface: prices live inside fact values in both (data), and LAST TOUCHED is a relative duration in both.

COULD NOT BE JUDGED FROM THE FRAME — needs a live check: the build fixture carries 8 facts against the prototype's 6, so the add row, the ✦ note and the record line all fall below the fold in the build frame. I confirmed all three exist and are correctly ordered in BoldCoreItem/BoldItemPage, but their rendered spacing, the note's wrapped height and the record line's exact position have not been seen at 1440×900. Re-shoot with a 6-fact fixture, or scrolled, to close that.

DATA, NOT DESIGN (listed so they are not mistaken for deviations): 8 vs 14 facts, 8 vs 2 gaps, \"today / Do you take my insurance?\" vs \"6d / financing terms\", question-shaped fact labels with trailing full stops vs the prototype's noun labels without, and the whole left rail (workspace name truncated to \"Dem…\", \"1 elsewhere\" vs \"3 elsewhere\", campaign values, \"D\" vs \"B\" core tile).

OUT OF THIS SURFACE, worth a separate row on their own pairs: (1) the build renders `.cvb-canvas-tail` — a 19px rotated square with two hairlines at the canvas right edge, y≈475 — which has no counterpart anywhere in Console Bold.dc.html; (2) the build's rail shows a \"«\" collapse control and drops the prototype's \"WORKSPACE · 1 OF 3\" mono line; (3) the build renders the add row on ALL FOUR tabs, but the prototype's `hasAdd` list (['fields','answers','facts','sources','gaps','email','num','people']) deliberately excludes `who`, so the prototype's \"Who you are\" tab has no \"Add a field\" row — that belongs on the core-who pair. Step counters: not applicable to this surface; the edit drawer this surface opens carries no StepDots, while the add-fact drawer (its own pair) does."}

|---|---|---|---|
| **Fact row sub-line — composition and affordance (the value she quotes)** | The sub-line IS the edit surface: `mkRows('facts')` sets `isEditable: true`, so `plainSub` is false and the row renders an `<input>` instead of a text div — IBM Plex Sans 12px, colour #3E4B44 (ink-soft), `border-bottom: 1px dashed #DCDFDD`, padding 4px 0 3px, margin-top 3px, dashed rule running the full flex width up to the chip. Row cursor is `text`. Typing commits to `factEdit` state; there is no other edit path. Row pitch measures 76px in the frame (Console Bold.dc.html lines 2337-2342, 4731-4740). | Sub-line is a static `<div>` — 11.5px, `var(--cvb-faint)` #8B968F, marginTop 3, line-height 1.45, no underline, no input, not selectable-as-editable. Row pitch measures 70px. The dashed-underline signature that says "this line is the thing you change" is gone entirely (bold-settings-kit.tsx RowList; BoldCoreItem.tsx factRows). | 🟡 spec-amend<br><sub>SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md line 18 ("every add/edit in settings opens the same right drawer, never an inline form or a modal") and lines 104-105 ("Each row opens an edit drawer (same shape as add, pre-filled)"). The build followed the spec; the spec contradicts the prototype's inline-editable row.</sub> |
| **Chevron on the fact row** | No chevron. The `vItem` row template emits, in order: optional spine / avatar / icon, the name+sub block, then chip / val / toggle — and stops. Fact rows carry `go: () => {}` (a no-op) and `cur: 'text'`, so nothing on the row is a navigation affordance (lines 2324-2354, 4736-4740). | A ghost chevron `›` — fontSize 12, `var(--cvb-ghost)` #C3CBC6, `flex: none` — is appended after the chip on every clickable row, and the row is `cursor: pointer` (bold-settings-kit.tsx RowList, `{clickable ? <span>›</span> : null}`). ## Business core → Gaps

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-core-gaps-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-core-gaps-1440x900.png`

SEED DIFFERENCE, NOT A DEVIATION: the frames run different data — prototype is Bright Smile Dental (14 facts / 2 gaps / LAST TOUCHED 6d "financing terms"), build is 8 facts / 8 gaps / "today" "Do you take my insurance?". Absolute values and gap names are not comparable. The row-count difference alone pushes the add row, the TabNote, the ✦ note and the record line below the fold in build-core-gaps; I confirmed all four actually render by reading build-drawer-answer-1440x900.png, which shows the same page scrolled behind the drawer.

NOT RAISED (reads as part of the settled attribution change): the sub-line's second clause differs — prototype "— she deflects every one", build "— she deflects until you answer". With no ask count in the first clause, "every one" has no antecedent, so the rewrite is the honest consequence of the settled change rather than a separate deviation. The clause ORDER (attribution — behaviour, em-dash joined) is identical in both.

MISSING FRAME PAIR: MANIFEST.json ships build-drawer-answer-1440x900.png with NO proto-drawer-answer counterpart, so the answer-a-gap drawer would go undiffed if nobody picked it up here. Rows 15–23 diff it against the nearest prototype truth: proto-drawer-fact-1440x900.png plus ADDFLOW.addfact and the shared overlay chassis at Console Bold.dc.html:1596 (the prototype has no gap-specific drawer variant — the gaps tab opens the plain add-fact flow).

SHELL, NOT THIS SURFACE (flag for the shell pass, all visible in this frame): (a) the build adds a « collapse control inside the workspace row, which squeezes the ws card until the name truncates to "Dem…" and the mono "WORKSPACE · 1 OF 3" line collapses to "WORKSP…" — the prototype shows both lines in full; (b) prototype campaign rows carry a right-hand value (8/12, $6.4k, $4.1k) and the Site agent row carries $33.6k, the build's carry none; (c) the build canvas grows a .cvb-canvas-tail diamond at x≈1345 pointing at the active dock tile — the prototype has no such element anywhere.

COULD NOT BE JUDGED FROM THE FRAMES: row and add-row hover states (read from source instead — row hover is missing in the build); the post-write toast (prototype flashes "She knows it now", build "She knows it now." with a trailing period — trivial, not filed as a row); whether the prototype's inline gap edit ever persisted (it writes to component state only, so the build's drawer is the only one that actually writes); and the zero-gap state — the prototype has no empty-gaps case, so the build's EmptyTab line and its "Nothing missing" forest pill have no prototype to be checked against and should be reviewed on their own merits.

## Business core → Who you are

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-core-who-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-core-who-1440x900.png`

WHAT MATCHES (checked, not deviations): neither prototype nor build puts a leading icon tile on a Who-you-are row — the `who` branch of `mkRows` sets no `hasIcon`, and `RowList` has no icon slot; correct on both sides. The `Core` chip is identical in both: 10px/700, radius 999, padding 3px 9px, fg #146B33 / bg #EAF5EE / border #CFE8D8 (build `CHIP.live` resolves to the same three tokens). Row name type matches (13.5px, weight 700, -.016em). Row padding (15px 4px) and the 40px content gutter match. Tab row matches exactly (14px, 800/500, paddingBottom 13, 2px forest underline, #F1F2F1 hairline). Status pill matches (10.5px/700, 6px 12px, amber triple #8a6d1a / #f7efda / #ead9a8). The ✦ card matches: #EAF5EE on #CFE8D8, radius 20, padding 18px 20px, marginTop 28, ✦ forest 13px, body 13px #1D5B34, forest button 12.5px/800 radius 12 padding 11px 16px marginTop 14. The `AddRow` atom itself is a faithful port (32px dashed tile radius 11, cyan 13px/700 label, padding 18px 4px) — only its presence on this tab is at issue. The eyebrow/title/back-arrow head matches (mono 9.5 .18em; display 900, -.032em, ink→forest gradient clip; 34px back tile radius 12).

WHAT COULD NOT BE JUDGED FROM THE FRAME: the two frames run different data (prototype 14 facts / 2 gaps / LAST TOUCHED 6d "financing terms"; build 8 / 8 / "today" "Do you take my insurance?"), so counts, the ✦ note body, and which identity fields happen to exist are not comparable — I diffed the row/registry SHAPE instead. The build's "today" and "—" LAST TOUCHED formats have no prototype precedent (the prototype only ever shows "6d"); worth an owner glance but not raised as a row. The drawer rows (container, step counter, button placement) were read from source only — no drawer is open in either frame; they need a live check at 1440×900 with "Add a field" open. The build's identity-row edit path (`EditFactDrawer`) has no prototype counterpart at all, so there is nothing to diff it against beyond "the prototype edits inline".

SHELL DIFFERENCES VISIBLE IN THIS FRAME BUT OUTSIDE THE SURFACE (not raised as rows — they will recur in every settings pair): the build's rail puts ADA SUGGESTS *above* ALWAYS ON, the prototype puts it below; the build's workspace chip truncates both the name ("Dem…") and the eyebrow ("WORKSP…") and drops the prototype's "1 OF 3" line while adding a « collapse control; the prototype's rail campaign rows and always-on rows carry money values ($6.4k, $33.6k) the build's do not; the build has an extra "›" peek handle at the canvas's right edge (x≈1345, y≈475) with no prototype counterpart; the prototype's dock chat tile carries a green unread dot the build's does not.

The dashed rule under every value is visible in the frame under all four rows. The value is only editable by clicking the row open into `EditFactDrawer`. Frame shows both sub-lines as plain grey text with no dashed rule. | 🔴 fix |

The extra 6px, plus the taller value, pushes the tab row and the whole tab body ~10px down the canvas (tab hairline y=208 → y=218). | 🔴 fix |
| **Tab-body top padding** | `padding:26px 40px 0` on the item content block. | `padding:22px 40px 40px` (BoldItemPage.tsx) — 4px less air between the tab row and the first row. | 🔴 fix |
| **Identity set · rows the build cannot render** | `ITEMS['ws:core'].who` opens with two rows that are the business itself: "Bright Smile Dental / Implants and orthodontics · Austin, TX" and "Two locations, 11 staff / Founded 2016". | `IDENTITY_KEYS = {offer, usp, icp, services, company_address, tone}` (BoldCoreItem.tsx:37) — there is no context-field key for the business name/category/city or for size/founded, so neither row can ever appear on this tab regardless of data. Spec line 110 also names them ("identity rows (business, size/founded, what you sell, who you want)"). | 🔴 fix |
| **Identity set · rows the build adds** | Exactly four identity rows; none of them an address, a writing voice, a services list or a differentiator. | `company_address` ("Company postal address", visible in the frame with a full street address), and also `tone` ("Writing voice"), `services` ("Services") and `usp` ("What makes you different") are admitted into the Who-you-are tab. The postal address in particular changes the tab from an identity read-back into a details form. | 🔴 fix |
| **ICP row label** | "Who you want" — sub "Adults 35+ within 15 miles, insurance optional". | `CONTEXT_FIELD_META.icp.label` = "Who to target" (packages/core/src/context.ts:133), so the same row reads "Who to target". (Not visible in this frame — the seeded workspace has no icp value — but it is what the build would render.) | 🔴 fix |
| **Identity row order** | Fixed authored order: business → size/founded → what you sell → who you want. | No ordering at all — `identity = fields.filter(...)` preserves `Object.entries(raw)` order from the context read, so the order is whatever the API happens to return. The frame shows "What you sell" then "Company postal address"; a different write order would reshuffle the tab. | 🔴 fix |
| **✦ note · action button label** | "Walk me through it" (`ITEMS['ws:core'].adaL`). | "Answer it now" — the handler jumps to the Gaps tab (`setTab(1)`) and opens the pre-filled add-fact drawer. (The note BODY differing is settled — gaps are attributed to the campaigns that need them — but the button wording is not covered by that ruling.) | 🔴 fix |
| **Canvas head · "?" tour button** | A 30×30 button, radius 11, border #E4E6E5, background #FCFCFC, ink #5A6660, glyph "?" 13px/700, sits immediately right of the status pill in the canvas head on every surface including this item page (Console Bold.dc.html:162). | Absent from the head — the head ends at the `8 gaps` pill. Help moved to `.cvb-help-launcher`: a 38px `position:fixed` circle at right 16 / bottom 16, forest fill, white "?", 0 6px 16px shadow, floating outside the canvas over the bottom-right of the viewport (apps/web/app/bold/bold.css:652). Shell-wide, not specific to this tab, but it changes this frame's head composition. | 🔴 fix |
| **Drawer container (where this tab's add/edit lands)** | `position:absolute; inset:0` INSIDE the canvas with a `rgba(10,14,12,.14)` scrim, so the rail and dock stay bright; panel is 392px, flat white, `border-left:1px solid #ECEDEC`, NO corner radius, padding 30px 28px (Console Bold.dc.html:1596-1597). | `SettingsDrawer` portals to `document.body`: `position:fixed` panel 424px wide, left corners rounded `var(--cvb-r-drawer)`=21px, `var(--cvb-gradient-panel)` fill, `var(--cvb-shadow-two-layer)`, over a full-page `var(--cvb-scrim)` that dims rail and dock too. The file's own comment claims this "matches the prototype's own overlays" — the prototype's overlay is canvas-scoped, so the rationale is wrong. Not visible in this frame; it is the container for both "Add a field" and the identity-row edit. | 🔴 fix |
| **Drawer step counter — form and placement** | A row of full-width 3px segment BARS (`flex:1`, gap 4, radius 2) sitting directly under the drawer title: done #146B33, current #CFE8D8, upcoming #EDEEED — a progress bar, no numerals (Console Bold.dc.html:1994-1998). | `StepDots` — despite the name, mono TEXT "1 OF 2" at 9.5px, .16em tracking, `var(--cvb-faint)`, sitting in the pinned footer between the Back button and the primary. | 🔴 fix |
| **Drawer button placement** | Buttons sit at the END of the scrolling body, 20px under the last field: a quiet "Back" pill (#F2F3F2, border #E4E6E5, 12px 15px) plus a `flex:1` full-width forest primary (#146B33, radius 12, padding 12px). | A pinned footer strip with its own `borderTop:1px solid var(--cvb-line-inner)` and padding 16/26/22 holds Back · "1 OF 2" · primary; the primary is auto-width, not full-bleed. | 🔴 fix |

## Business core → Where it comes from

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-core-sources-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-core-sources-1440x900.png`

MEASUREMENT CORRECTION: the leading icon tile is 32×32, not 28px — both the prototype markup (width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:11px, Console Bold.dc.html:2331-2333) and the pixels in the proto frame (x=317–348) say 32. The owner's "28px" was an eyeball; build it at 32 with an 11px radius and a 14px gap.

KIT-WIDE CONSEQUENCE: the icon omission is not local to this tab. `SettingsRow`/`RowList` in bold-settings-kit.tsx have no icon or avatar slot at all, while the prototype's mkRows gives leading tiles to senders (`✉` mint #EAF5EE/#CFE8D8/#146B33), numbers (`✆` sky #E2F3F6/#BFE3EB/#0E7D93), form fields (`≡` grey #F2F3F2/#E4E6E5/#8B968F) and 38px round avatars to people rows. Fixing this pair means adding the slot to the kit plus plum and (for other tabs) sky tint tokens, which packages/theme/src/console-bold.css does not define today — it has forest/mint, cyan, amber, danger, slate only.

SHARED-COMPONENT WARNING: the four stat-strip rows all land in `BoldMetaStrip` (bold-cards.tsx), which is used by other detail surfaces too. Its label/value/sub typography matches nothing in the prototype — the prototype's only strip of this shape is the item strip at Console Bold.dc.html:2056-2062. Changing it will move every surface that uses it; confirm against those pairs before landing.

NOT JUDGEABLE FROM THESE FRAMES — the source drawer. Clicking a source row in the build opens `SourceDrawer` (WHERE / LAST READ / FACTS FROM IT / CADENCE, a "Remove this source" text link in the body, and "Read it again" alone in a pinned footer, flex-spacer pushing it right). The prototype has NO source drawer — the row only fires a toast, `flash('brightsmile.com — 9 pages read, last Tuesday')`. So the drawer's shape came from SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md §5 line 116-117, not from the prototype. Its nearest prototype analogue is the sender drawer, which puts actions inside the body as tinted icon rows (`✉` mint "Send a test email", `◷` sky "Change the daily cap", `✕` danger "Remove this sender", Console Bold.dc.html:4856-4860) with the ✦ line and a labelled mono record line at the foot, and no pinned footer button. That mismatch needs a live check and probably an owner ruling; I did not raise it as a row because neither frame shows it.

DATA, NOT DEVIATION: the two frames are different tenants (prototype = Bright Smile, 4 sources / 14 facts / 2 gaps / LAST TOUCHED 6d "financing terms"; build = a dental demo workspace, 2 sources / 8 facts / 8 gaps / LAST TOUCHED today "Do you take my insurance?"). Source names, counts, the LAST TOUCHED sub, and the ✦ note's sentence were treated as data and not reported. The build's ✦ note is derived and its button performs a real write, which matches the settled ruling on campaign-attributed gaps.

OUT OF SCOPE (belongs to the shell pair): left-rail differences — the build's `«` collapse button, "1 elsewhere" vs "3 elsewhere", campaign rows with `✓` state discs vs the prototype's mixed dot/value rows, the missing "WORKSPACE · 1 OF 3" line under the workspace name, ADA SUGGESTS count, and the right-edge `›` panel handle at x≈1345. Also verified matching and NOT reported: tab row (gap 22, padding 22px 40px 0, 14px 800/500, 2px forest underline, 13px padding-bottom), row metrics (padding 15px 4px, gap 14, name 13.5/700/-.016em, sub 11.5/#8B968F/marginTop 3), chip geometry (10px/700, radius 999, 3px 9px), the dashed AddRow (32px tile, 11px radius, dashed #CFD6D1, `+` 15px, cyan 13px/700 label), and the ✦ note (mint #EAF5EE on #CFE8D8, radius 20, padding 18px 20px, marginTop 28, forest solid button 12.5/800, radius 12, 11px 16px, marginTop 14) — all pixel-for-value identical.

The hover-state row was confirmed from source only; it cannot be seen in a static frame.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Source row · leading icon tile** | Every source row opens with a 32×32 tinted tile, 11px radius, 14px gap before the name, glyph at 13px: `◍` for a website/profile source, `◫` when the name ends `.pdf`/`.docx` (Console Bold.dc.html:4743-4747). Tint is the plum triple — fill #F0EDF9, border #DCD5EF, glyph #5B4A8A. Measured in the frame: tile spans x=317–348 (32px), fill (240,237,249), border (220,213,239); the name starts at x=364. | No tile at all. `SettingsRow` in bold-settings-kit.tsx has no icon field and `RowList` renders name+sub flush at the row's left edge — name starts at x=318, i.e. 46px left of the prototype. packages/theme also defines no plum tokens (no --cvb-plum* anywhere in console-bold.css), so the fix needs the tint added as well as the slot. | 🔴 fix |

Document rows: `Uploaded by you · 14 prices found`. Connector row: `Hours and location, kept in sync`. | cadence · yield only — `Read weekly · 3 facts found`, `Uploaded by you · 2 facts found`. `sourceLine()` in BoldCoreItem.tsx never emits a date, even though the same row object carries `updatedAt` and the build's own source drawer prints it as LAST READ. Not an honesty gate: the date is in hand. | 🔴 fix |

## Senders → Email

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-senders-email-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-senders-email-1440x900.png`

STATE, NOT DEVIATION (checked in source, deliberately not listed as rows): the build frame is seeded with two unauthenticated .test senders, no numbers and a 20% ramp, so the header pill reads amber \"Needs a look\" instead of mint \"All verified\" (the build has the identical `All verified` / live branch), the chips read \"Unchecked\", NUMBERS reads 0, and the ✦ note is the unverified-sender branch rather than the ramp branch. Row/stat/chip tone differences that follow purely from that seed were not raised; what I did raise is the shape the build would render in ANY state.

SETTLED, NOT RAISED, as instructed: typed wells for guardrail caps, the credits hero's USED/ADDED, the \"Nothing has drawn down credits this month\" line, the forest Top up button with its 34%-white hairline, \"Member\" as a human's role, and gap attribution to campaigns.

SHELL-LEVEL, VISIBLE IN THE FRAME BUT NOT OWNED BY THIS SURFACE (flagging rather than filing against Senders): (1) the prototype's canvas head carries a 30×30 \"?\" tour button to the right of the status pill; the build's head has none and a floating circular \"?\" launcher sits at the bottom-right outside the canvas instead. (2) The build's rail adds a \"«\" collapse button that squeezes the workspace name to \"Dem…\" and the eyebrow to \"WORKSP…\", where the prototype shows \"Bright Smile\" and \"WORKSPACE · 1 OF 3\" in full. (3) The rail block order differs — prototype is ALWAYS ON → ADA SUGGESTS → Business core; build is ADA SUGGESTS → ALWAYS ON → Business core. (4) A stray \"›\" peek handle appears at the canvas's right edge (~x1345, y475) in the build with no prototype counterpart. All four should be diffed in the shell pass, not fixed inside BoldSendersItem.

FRAME VS SOURCE: rows 1-15 are confirmed in both the frames and the source. Rows 16-27 (the add-email-sender flow and the per-sender drawer) are source-verified only — neither overlay is open in the supplied 1440×900 frames, so their geometry should be re-checked against fresh drawer frames before the fixes are signed off. In particular the empty-space question cannot be judged from these frames: the build's 424px drawer with a pinned footer needs a captured shot to confirm the step-1 and step-3 content does not leave a dead band above the footer.

BUILD FILES: apps/web/components/bold/settings/BoldSendersItem.tsx (rows, stats, ✦ note), apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx (RowList/AddRow/CHIP/SettingsDrawer/StepDots/AdaNote/RecordLine), apps/web/components/bold/bold-cards.tsx (BoldMetaStrip), apps/web/components/bold/settings/BoldItemPage.tsx (tab row, TabNote, paddings), apps/web/components/bold/settings/BoldSenderDrawers.tsx (sender + add drawers), apps/web/components/bold/settings/settings-data.ts (dnsPosture, warmupPercent). Prototype: design_handoff_console_v3/prototypes/Console Bold.dc.html — ITEMS['ws:senders'] at 4520-4540, mkRows email branch at 4718-4730, row template at 2323-2364, stat strip at 2054-2063, overlay/add drawer at 1595-1597 and 1986-2038, ADDFLOW at 4865-4917.

## Senders → Numbers

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-senders-num-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-senders-num-1440x900.png`

MISSING DATA vs MISSING SURFACE, called explicitly: the absence of the "+1 512 555 0142" ROW is missing data (the demo workspace has no TWILIO_SMS sender) — but its composition when data exists is a surface deviation (no icon tile, chevron added, sub-line rewritten to volume). The A2P registration row is missing surface for a live number (the build only ever renders A2P inside a request row's sub-line). The Voice toggle is missing surface outright — no code path renders it with or without data. The status pill reading "Needs a look" (amber) instead of "All verified" (mint) is pure data: the build's own ternary produces "All verified" when every sender's DNS passes. The empty-tab line and the ✦ note copy are likewise data-derived and not listed as deviations.

Verified against source, not the frame: everything about the add-a-number drawer and the number sender drawer (rows 20–33) — neither is open in either screenshot. Worth a live pass with a seeded number and a seeded request to confirm the duplicate-row and stale-"Requested"-chip findings, which I read off the code rather than saw.

Out of this surface's scope but visible in the frames, so flagging rather than filing: the prototype puts the "?" (take the tour) button in the canvas head at top-right, 30×30 radius 11 with a #E4E6E5 hairline, next to the status pill; the build has no head button and instead floats `.cvb-help-launcher` — a 38px solid-forest circle — fixed at the bottom-right of the viewport. The build also adds a rail-collapse « button and a `.cvb-canvas-tail` notch on the canvas's right edge that the prototype has neither of. All three are shell, not Senders.

Two things I could not judge from a static frame: row hover (the prototype rows carry `style-hover="background:#FBFCFB"`; `RowList` has no hover state at all — a clickable row that does not respond is worth a live check), and the drawer entry animation (prototype `bOver .32s`, build `cvb-over .32s` — same curve, same duration, likely fine).

One knock-on to weigh before fixing row 19: `--cvb-scrim` is a shared theme token in packages/theme/src/console-bold.css, so lightening it to the prototype's .14 changes every overlay in the console, not just this drawer.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Number/A2P row — leading icon tile** | Every `chip` row in the num tab carries a leading 32×32 tile, border-radius 11, `gap:14` before the title: background #E2F3F6, border 1px #BFE3EB, glyph ✆ at 13px in #0E7D93 (the sky/cyan triple). Set in mkRows: `hasIcon:true, ic: kind==='num' ? '✆' : '✉', bg:'#E2F3F6', bd:'#BFE3EB', fg:'#0E7D93'` (Console Bold.dc.html ~4724), rendered by the `r.hasIcon` branch at line 2331. | `RowList` in apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx has no icon branch at all — the row's first child is the title/sub block. No tile, no tint, on any row type, on any settings item page. MISSING SURFACE, not missing data: a seeded number would still render tile-less. | 🔴 fix |
| **Row trailing chevron** | No chevron anywhere on item-page rows. Affordance is `cursor:pointer` + a `#FBFCFB` hover only. The row ends at the chip / value / toggle. | `RowList` appends `<span>›</span>` at fontSize 12 in `var(--cvb-ghost)` to every row where `onOpen` is a function — so the number row and the request row both get a chevron the prototype does not have. ADDED SURFACE. | 🔴 fix |
| **Voice toggle row (3rd row of the tab)** | Third row: title "Voice", sub "Outbound calls and voicemail drops", NO leading tile (the `tg` branch never sets hasIcon, so the title sits flush left at the row's edge — visibly ~47px left of the two rows above it), and a 44×26 pill toggle ON (#146B33, knob at left:21px) on the right. `['Voice','Outbound calls and voicemail drops','tg',true]`. | No voice row exists in any code path. `numberRows` = active SMS senders + non-cancelled requests, nothing else (BoldSendersItem.tsx). MISSING SURFACE — it is absent with data too. `carries: 'sms' \| 'sms_voice'` is on NumberRequestRow so the state exists; there is no write endpoint for flipping it. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>also required by SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md line 146 ("and a `tg` for voice")</sub> |
| **A2P registration row (2nd row of the tab)** | A standalone row of its own: sky ✆ tile, title "A2P registration", sub "Brand and campaign both approved", chip "Live" (mint #146B33 / #EAF5EE / #CFE8D8) on the right. Inert — `isSender` is false for it, so cursor is `default` and it opens nothing. | No standalone A2P row. `a2pState` appears only inside a REQUEST row's sub-line (`A2P not filed yet` / `A2P <state>`), so once a number is a live sender the A2P state vanishes from the tab entirely. MISSING SURFACE for the live-number state (partly missing data for the demo workspace, which has neither). | 🔴 fix<br><sub>also required by SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md line 145–146 ("A2P registration state")</sub> |
| **Number row sub-line composition** | `SMS and voice · Austin local` — what the line carries, then where it is local to. Capability + locality. | `${s.sentToday ?? 0} sent today${s.dailyLimit ? ` of ${s.dailyLimit}` : ''}` → e.g. "0 sent today of 200". Volume, not capability or locality — a different fact in a different order. Both inputs exist (`carries` and `areaCode` on NumberRequestRow), so this is not a data gap. | 🔴 fix |
| **Number row title** | `+1 512 555 0142` — the number, space-grouped. | `s.fromEmail ?? "This number"` — the raw value of a field named fromEmail, unformatted, with a literal noun placeholder ("This number") when it is absent. The prototype never shows a placeholder in the title slot. | 🔴 fix |
| **Request row state chip** | The registration row's chip carries its real state ("Live" when brand and campaign are approved). | `chip: "Requested", tone: "warn"` is hard-coded on every request row regardless of `r.status`, so a RESERVED or ACTIVE request still reads "Requested" in amber. Build defect, independent of the prototype. | 🔴 fix |
| **Duplicate row once a number goes live** | One row per number. | `requests` filters only `status !== "CANCELLED"`, so an ACTIVE request and the TWILIO_SMS sender it produced both render — two rows for one number, one of them permanently chipped "Requested". | 🔴 fix |
| **Stat 1 label** | `EMAIL DOMAINS  2  both authenticated`. | `EMAIL SENDERS  2  0 of 2 authenticated`. The sub form is fine (it is the build's `${authed} of ${email.length}` template with real numbers, and it says "all authenticated" when all pass), but the label counts senders where the prototype and the spec count domains. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>prototype and SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md line 138 agree on EMAIL DOMAINS; distinct domains are derivable from the sender addresses</sub> |
| **Stat 2 sub-line** | `NUMBERS 1 · local Austin area code` — the locality of the number. | `sending and receiving` when a number exists. | **Stat 3 sub-line — the warm-up ETA** | `WARM-UP 82% · full volume in 4 days` — a stated finish date/ETA. | `WARM-UP 20% · still ramping` — no ETA. Only the `pct === null` case ("not started") is genuinely unstatable. | 🔴 fix |

Smaller labels, heavier and larger values, groups pushed twice as far apart. Container padding, background and hairlines do match. | 🔴 fix |
| **Last row hairline** | The `sc-for` puts `border-bottom:1px solid #F4F5F4` on every row including the last, so the list closes on a hairline that separates it from the add row — visible under "Voice" in the frame. | `RowList` suppresses it: `borderBottom: last ? ## Senders → Health

> ⚠️ **No frame pair.** `build-senders-health-1440x900.png` has no `proto-` counterpart — diffed live against the prototype source, which is cited per row.

OUTCOME: (A) — MISSING PAIR, not a build-only surface. `ITEMS['ws:senders'].health` is live prototype data at `Console Bold.dc.html:4533-4538`, and `tabs` at line 4522 lists `['health','Health']` alongside email and num. Health is not special-cased in `mkRows`, so it falls to the generic fallback (lines 4788-4806) and renders through the same `it.isRows` markup (lines 2322-2355) that produced the captured proto-senders-email frame. Same stat strip, same ✦ card, same page. It was simply never shot. SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md §6 line 147 independently names the same four items, so prototype and spec agree here — none of the rows above is a spec-amend on that point.

CONFIDENCE / WHAT I COULD NOT SEE LIVE. There is no proto frame for this tab, so the prototype column is read from source rather than from pixels. Two consequences worth a live check before the fix lands:
(a) I traced the three `val` rows to the fallback branch at line 4803, where `sub` is overwritten with `r[3]` and `isEditable` is set — which means the descriptive halves ("Under 0.4% across both domains", "None in 30 days", "Rises as the new domain warms") are NOT rendered, and the value appears in the dashed inline-edit field instead. That is what the code does; I verified the dashed-field treatment against the captured proto-core-facts frame, which uses the same `isEditable` path. But rendering a read-only metric like bounce rate as an editable field looks like a prototype defect rather than intent. If the owner reads it that way, the honest port is name + descriptive sub + right-aligned mono value (the `t:"val"` shape the kit already has, as used for Reply-to on the Email tab), with inline edit kept only for Daily ceiling. Flag it and get a ruling rather than porting the quirk.
(b) The last-row divider and the #F4F5F4/#F1F2F1 delta are code-level reads; the missing last-row hairline is visible in the build frame, the 3-value colour delta is not visible at 1440x900.

DEAD SPACE — not filed as a row. The build leaves ~200px of empty canvas below the record line, against ~150px in proto-senders-email. The gap is a consequence of the tab carrying two rows where the prototype carries four; restoring the row set closes it. Not an independent layout defect.

OUT OF SCOPE, deliberately not filed. The rail focus capsule `«`, the canvas-edge `›` at (1345,475), and the differing seed data (test-domain addresses, 20% warm-up, "Needs a look" vs "All verified") are shell chrome and live data, shared across all 21 pairs. The ✦ AdaNote is a byte-for-byte match to the prototype (mint #EAF5EE / #CFE8D8, radius 20, 18px/20px padding, ✦ forest, 12.5px/800 forest button at radius 12, padding 11/16, marginTop 14) — nothing to report. Tab row, tab underline and active-weight all match. The absence of an Add row on this tab is correct: the prototype's `hasAdd` list at line 4964 excludes `health`.

ONE THING THE BUILD GOT RIGHT, do not "fix" it. `EmptyTab` — "Health arrives with your first sender." — has no prototype counterpart (the prototype has no empty states at all) and is not in this frame, but it is the right instinct and should survive the row-set fix.

REDUNDANCY WORTH NAMING. Spec §6 already puts per-sender bounces and complaints in the sender drawer ("three stats: sent 30d / bounces / complaints"). So the build's Health tab currently duplicates the drawer's job while leaving the workspace-level view the tab exists for unbuilt — which is the strongest argument that row 1's verdict is `fix` rather than adopt.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Health tab row set (the whole tab body)** | Four fixed rows, in order: `Bounce rate` / 0.4% · `Spam complaints` / 0 · `Daily ceiling` / 320 · `Pause if bounces exceed 2%` with sub "She stops rather than burn the domain" and a toggle ON. A workspace-level deliverability read-out. | One row per sender (2 rows) — `hello@brightsmile.test`, `team@brightsmile.test` — each with sends-today, sweep state and a state chip. None of the prototype's four items appear. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Not an honesty gate: every one of the four is computable from the shipped engine (DEC-078 — bounce/spam rates are the penalty model's own inputs, the 2% bound is a HEALTH_SIGNALS constant, effective ceiling = min(curve, dailyLimit)). Where a sweep has not run, say so in the prototype's shape ("No sweep yet" in the value slot), which is the credits honesty pattern REDO §2 keeps.</sub> |
| **Deliverability numbers on the deliverability tab** | Bounce rate and spam-complaint count are the tab's two headline values (0.4%, 0). | Neither appears anywhere on the tab. No health score either — both rows read "no health sweep has run yet". The Health tab renders zero deliverability numbers. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>The Health analogue of REDO §1.1's ruling — a buy screen with no prices. If the sweep genuinely has not run, the rows must still exist and state the absence in place of the value.</sub> |
| **The ruling toggle row `Pause if bounces exceed 2%`** | A `tg` row at the foot of the tab, ON, 44x26 forest toggle right-aligned, sub "She stops rather than burn the domain". | Absent. The tab carries no toggle and no statement that auto-pause exists. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>The kit already has the honest rendering for a rail the schema enforces — `SettingsRow t:"tg"` with `locked: true`, which shows an "Always on" chip instead of a flippable toggle. ## Team and roles → People

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-team-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-team-1440x900.png`

FIXTURE DIVERGENCE (not deviations): the frames are shot against different data — prototype is Bright Smile with 2 humans / 1 owner / no invites; build is a 3-human, 2-owner workspace. So PEOPLE 2→3, OWNERS 1→2, and the extra "Dr. Ines Duarte" row are data, not build defects. I judged every row above on composition and style, not on values.

CORRECTLY MATCHING — checked in source and deliberately NOT raised: no leading icon tile and no avatar on People rows in either (the prototype's people rows take the generic mkRows branch at Console Bold.dc.html:4776, which sets neither `hasIcon` nor `hasAvatar`; RowList likewise renders no leading slot — so the absence is right, not an omission). Row geometry (flex, gap 14, padding 15px 4px), name type (13.5px/700/-.016em/1.4) and sub type (11.5px, #8b968f, margin-top 3, 1.45) match to the decimal. Chip geometry matches (10px/700, radius 999, padding 3px 9px). The dashed "Invite someone" add row matches exactly — 32×32 icon, radius 11, 1px dashed #CFD6D1, cyan #0E7D93 13px/700 label, padding 18px 4px, gap 12 — and sits in the right order (rows → add row → ✦ band). Tab strip matches (gap 22, padding 22px 40px 0, 14px, 800/500, 2px forest underline, #8b968f inactive). Status-pill chrome matches. The ✦ band's own chrome and its forest-solid in-band button match to the pixel; only the derivation that feeds it is missing. Everything on this surface is inline on the item page in both — no modal, no drawer, no step counter — so container type, step counts, prices/rates/dates and pinned-footer questions do not arise on the People tab.

WHAT ROLES CAN DO (second tab, not visible in either frame — read from source, so listed here rather than as rows): the prototype's role rows carry state chips Core / Live / Sent / Sent with short scopes ("Owner · Billing, sub-accounts, everything below", "Member · Inbox and contacts only", Console Bold.dc.html:4549-4553); the build replaces the chip with a computed head-count pill `"N here"` toned live/mute and swaps in the long WORKSPACE_ROLE_SCOPE sentences (BoldTeamItem.tsx:170-177), then appends a mono all-caps TabNote ("THESE ARE THE ROLES THIS PLATFORM ENFORCES…") the prototype has no counterpart for. Worth a second pass if that tab is in scope.

SHELL CHROME visible in the frame but outside this surface: the build's rail truncates the workspace name to "Dem…" and its eyebrow to "WORKSP…" where the prototype shows "Bright Smile" over "WORKSPACE · 1 OF 3", and the build adds a "«" collapse control the prototype does not have. Belongs to a rail pair, not to Team → People.

NEEDS A LIVE CHECK, not judgeable from a still: (1) whether the ✦ band reappears once a lapsed invite or lone-owner condition exists — the two shipped derivations were not exercised by this fixture; (2) whether pending/expired invite rows (BoldTeamItem.tsx:151-160, chips Pending/Expired) render at the right tone and position, since this workspace has none; (3) the row hover state, which a static frame cannot show — I read its absence from bold-settings-kit.tsx rather than from the image.

## Guardrails → Sending limits

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-guard-sending-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-guard-sending-1440x900.png`

SETTLED / NOT RAISED, as instructed: typed recessed wells instead of steppers (DEC-134); the guardrail caps' well styling (--cvb-well-fill-2 #F4F6F5 / --cvb-well-line-2 #DFE3E1 / inset shadow, radius 12, 84px wide, right-aligned mono 12.5px) matches the §2 well contract. Also not raised as deviations because they are pure seed-data differences: LIMITS ON 7→4, CAMPAIGNS INHERITING 4→5, OVERRIDES 1→0, the sidebar campaign names/values, and the prototype's Ada card naming a specific 11:20 SMS-ceiling event the empty build workspace has no equivalent for.

THINGS THE BRIEF ASKED ABOUT THAT ARE NOT DEVIATIONS HERE: leading icons/tints — the prototype's `sending` rows carry none (mkRows only attaches the 32×32 radius-11 tinted tile for the `email`/`num`/`sources`/`fields` kinds), and the build attaches none, so there is nothing to match; chevrons — no chevron on either side of this tab; container type — inline rows on both sides, no modal or drawer is involved in Sending limits; step counts/counters — none on this surface; price/rate/date — none shown on either side; button placement — the only button on the tab is the ✦ card's action, inside the card in both (see the row above); the header status pill's own geometry and live tone (10.5px/700, radius 999, padding 6px 12px, margin-top 9, forest on mint with #CFE8D8 hairline) matches exactly — only the copy differs; the ✦ card itself matches on fill (#EAF5EE), hairline (#CFE8D8), radius 20, padding 18px 20px, margin-top 28, forest ✦ at 13px, body 13px/#1D5B34/lh 1.6; the add row is correctly absent (prototype's hasAdd excludes 'sending'; SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md §11 line 294 gives guard tabs no add label); tab row geometry (gap 22, padding 22px 40px 0, 14px labels, 800/500 weights, 2px forest underline, padding-bottom 13, margin-bottom -1) matches.

OUT OF THIS SURFACE, VISIBLE IN THE FRAMES — worth a separate pass, not filed as rows here: (1) the rail's block ORDER is swapped — prototype is ALWAYS ON / INBOUND then ✦ ADA SUGGESTS; the build puts ADA SUGGESTS above ALWAYS ON. (2) The build's rail workspace card adds a « collapse button that squeezes the name to 'Dem…' and the kind line to 'WORKSP…', and drops the prototype's 'WORKSPACE · 1 OF 3' counter. (3) The white canvas-tail notch on the canvas right edge at y≈475 exists only in the build — but that is a console-wide B0 adoption from the legacy canon (Q-063), not a per-surface deviation, so I left it alone.

CROSS-TAB, FOUND IN SOURCE WHILE CONFIRMING THIS TAB (would need its own frames to file properly): 'Campaign overrides' — the prototype's `over` rows are inert chip rows with no chevron (cur:'default'), while the build's RowList makes each override row clickable and appends a ghost '›' chevron. 'What she may say' — the prototype has 2 chip rows pilled 'Locked' plus 2 live toggles; the build renders 4 rows all pilled 'Always on' in the mint CHIP.live style with no toggles, and the row set is different copy entirely.

NEEDS A LIVE CHECK, NOT JUDGEABLE FROM THE FRAME: whether the greyed '200' in the email well is genuinely the placeholder (the code says yes — caps.email is '' when nothing is stored) rather than a stored value; whether the LIMITS ON count is meant to include the 4 ALWAYS_ON rails that live on a different tab (limitsOn = dailyCap keys + ALWAYS_ON.length + sendingWindow, which is why an untouched workspace reads 4 rather than 0); and the row hover tint, which a static frame can only confirm on the one row the prototype capture happened to have the cursor over.

## Guardrails → Campaign overrides

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-guard-over-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-guard-over-1440x900.png`

MATCHES (checked explicitly, no deviation): leading icons — neither side puts an icon tile, avatar or spine on an override row; the prototype's `over` rows go through the generic branch of mkRows, which sets no hasIcon, and the build's RowList has no leading slot at all. Titles start flush at x=316 in both. · Container type — the overrides list is inline in the canvas in BOTH; no modal, no drawer, no sheet, so no empty-drawer problem here. · Step counters — none on this surface in either. · Price / rate / date — none rendered on this surface in either. · Row geometry — padding 15px 4px, gap 14, title 13.5px/700/-.016em, sub 11.5px #8B968F marginTop 3: identical. Measured row pitch 69.5px (proto) vs 70px (build). · Chip metrics — 10px/700, radius 999, padding 3px 9px: identical. · ✦ card — mint #EAF5EE on #CFE8D8, radius 20, padding 18px 20px, marginTop 28, ✦ glyph forest 13px, body 13px #1D5B34/1.6: identical, and measured span x=313→1302 on both. · Tab row — gap 22, padding 22px 40px 0, 14px labels, 800/500 weight flip, 2px forest underline: identical. · Meta strip shell — padding 12px 40px on #FCFCFC with #F1F2F1 top and bottom borders: identical.

DATA, NOT FIDELITY: the stat numbers differ because the fixtures differ (proto 7/4/1 on four campaigns, build 4/5/0 on five). Worth flagging separately that LIMITS ON is derived differently — the build computes dailyCap keys + 4 ALWAYS_ON rails + (sendingWindow ? 1 : 0) (BoldGuardItem.tsx:157-158), which can never reach the prototype's 7 on the build's own three-cap Sending limits tab; that belongs to the Sending-limits diff, not this one.

OUT OF SURFACE, but visible in the frame and worth logging against the shell: (1) the build draws a `.cvb-canvas-tail` — a white 19px rotated square at the canvas right edge (~x=1345, y=475) pointing at the active dock tile — which the prototype has nowhere (grep for rotate(45deg) in Console Bold.dc.html returns nothing). (2) The build's rail adds a mint `«` collapse button in the workspace header that the prototype does not have; it squeezes the workspace card until the name and eyebrow truncate to "Dem…" / "WORKSP…". (3) The rail section ORDER is flipped: prototype is ALWAYS ON (y=493) → ADA SUGGESTS (y=630) → Business core; build is ADA SUGGESTS (y=471) → ALWAYS ON (y=548) → Business core. (4) The prototype's Site agent rail row carries a live dot and a "$33.6k" value; the build's carries neither.

COULD NOT JUDGE FROM THE FRAME — needs a live check: the departure state of this tab. The build fixture has zero departing campaigns, so the "Tighter" chip tone, the mid-dot departure sub-line, the departure-state TabNote, the amber OVERRIDES stat and the "Open <campaign>" ✦ action are all read from source (BoldGuardItem.tsx:137-149, 211-220, 378-383) rather than seen. Re-shoot this tab against a workspace with at least one campaign whose dailyCap or sendingWindow differs before signing those rows off. Also unverifiable from a still: the prototype's row hover tint (#FBFCFB, Console Bold.dc.html:2324) has no counterpart in the build's RowList, which sets cursor:pointer with no hover state — so the build's only click affordance is the chevron this report asks you to delete; add the hover tint in the same change.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Override row — trailing chevron** | No chevron on ANY item-page row, in any tab, on any ws:* page. The prototype's row template (Console Bold.dc.html:2324-2352) has exactly five optional trailing slots — hasChip / hasVal / hasTg (plus leading hasSpine / hasAvatar / hasIcon) — and no chevron element exists in it. The chip terminates the row: measured, the Default chip's right edge lands at x=1298, i.e. flush with the 40px content edge. | RowList (bold-settings-kit.tsx:534-536) appends `{clickable ? <span style={{fontSize:12,color:'var(--cvb-ghost)'}}>›</span> : null}` after the chip. Every override row is clickable, so all five carry it. Measured: chip right edge pulled in to x=1280, chevron glyph occupies x=1296-1298 in --cvb-ghost #C3CBC6. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Prototype is truth. Drop the › glyph; if the row stays clickable, carry the affordance the way the prototype does — cursor:pointer plus the row hover tint #FBFCFB — and let the chip end the row.</sub> |
| **Override row — "Default" chip fill** | Filled grey pill. 'Default' is not in the prototype's chip colour map, so it falls to the mute triple: fg #5A6660, bg #F2F3F2, bd #E4E6E5. ## Credits → Where they go

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-credits-usage-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-credits-usage-1440x900.png`

SETTLED, confirmed present and NOT reported: hero drops INCLUDED MONTHLY / BURN / RUNS OUT for USED/ADDED plus a stated reason (build renders exactly 2 tiles, divider at x=808; prototype renders 4, dividers at x=561/808/1056); the allowance bar and "59% of your monthly allowance left" replaced by "Your plan does not carry a monthly credit allowance yet, so there is no share of one to show."; "Nothing has drawn down credits this month." in place of the row list; Top up forest-solid with the 34%-white hairline (sampled fill rgb(20,107,51), hairline at x=1075/1076 and 1274/1275, white label).

VERIFIED MATCHING (no row raised): the 2px signature-gradient top edge is pixel-identical despite the angle differing on paper (proto `90deg`, build `--cvb-gradient-signature` at `135deg`) — sampled across x=416→1216 the two hairlines agree within 1/255 at every stop, because at 987×2 the 135° gradient line degenerates to horizontal. Hero body gradient, radius 22, padding 26/28, CREDITS LEFT eyebrow (9.5px/.18em/rgba(255,255,255,.5)), the 56px/900/-.042em balance, tile eyebrow (9px/.12em/.45 white) and tile value (20px/900/-.028em, #fff for the first tile and rgba(255,255,255,.86) for the rest), tab pill geometry (gap 5, padding 4, radius 13; pill padding 9/16, radius 10, #fff active on --cvb-ink, --cvb-faint inactive), the 22px gap hero→tabs and tabs→body, and the usage row's own metrics (gap 14, padding 14px 4px, 32×32/radius-11 tile, 13.5px/700/-.018em name, 11.5px #8B968F sub at marginTop 3, 120px bar column at 5px/radius 3 on #F1F2F1, 17px/900/-.026em figure) all match.

COULD NOT BE SETTLED FROM THE FRAME (needs a live check): the hero's tile band measures 73px tall in the prototype (divider y=299→bottom y=373) and 81px in the build (y=321→403), an 8px difference that the CSS does not explain — both use padding 15px 18px, marginTop 7, the same font sizes and no explicit line-height. Most likely a web-font metric or fallback difference (`'Schibsted Grotesk',sans-serif` inline vs `var(--cvb-font-display)`/`.cvb-display`, and `'IBM Plex Mono',monospace` vs `var(--cvb-font-mono)`), so check whether both faces actually loaded in the build capture before treating it as a spacing bug.

Also observed, filed as context rather than deviations: (1) the build's `EYEBROW` uses .18em tracking while the only in-tab eyebrow the prototype defines on this surface ("TOP-UPS BOUGHT", line 2609) uses .14em — not directly comparable, since the prototype has no eyebrow in the usage tab, but worth aligning when the Top-ups tab is diffed. (2) The prototype is internally inconsistent about Ada's tint on this surface: the usage row "Ada drafting" is amber (#F7EFDA/#EAD9A8/#8A6D1A) while the rates card "Anything Ada writes" is mint/forest — pick one when restoring the free row. (3) The rates grid track differs (`minmax(196px,1fr)` proto vs `minmax(220px,1fr)` build) but that is the "What things cost" tab, off this frame. (4) Rail content differs entirely (workspace name, campaign list, dollar figures, facts/gaps counts) and the rail is collapsed-capable in the build — seed data and shell, out of scope for this surface.

The prototype uses "Credits and usage" ONLY as the settings-hub card name (line 4308), never as the page title. | "Credits and usage" — apps/web/components/bold/bold-data.ts:61 `credits: ["WORKSPACE", "Credits and usage"]`. The hub-card name has been promoted to the page title. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Prototype is pixel truth (SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md line 3). The §9 heading "Credits and usage (surface `credits`)" names the surface key, not the rendered header string, so it does not license the change.</sub> |

## Credits → What things cost

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-credits-rates-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-credits-rates-1440x900.png`

Things that MATCH and were checked so they are not mistaken for gaps: card chrome is pixel-identical — background #FCFCFC (--cvb-panel-quiet), border 1px #ECEDEC (--cvb-line), radius 17, padding 17; icon tile 26×26, radius 9, 1px border, 12px glyph; name 12.5/700 at -.016em; figure 26/900 at -.032em; unit 11px #8B968F; sub-line 11.5px #8B968F, line-height 1.45, margin-top 9. Grid gap 12 and margin-top 22 match. Every tint triple the build shares with the prototype resolves to the identical hex: mint #EAF5EE/#CFE8D8/#146B33, cyan #E2F3F6/#BFE3EB/#0E7D93, slate #EAF3F5/#CFE4E9/#356170, violet #F0EDF9/#DCD5EF/#5B4A8A (hard-coded, correctly), amber #F7EFDA/#EAD9A8/#8A6D1A. Shared glyphs match exactly: ✉ email, ✆ SMS, ☎ call minute, ◉ enrichment, ✦ Ada writes. Sub-line wording for the four shared cards is verbatim. Unit pluralisation (credit/credits) behaves the same. Tab labels, order, default tab ("Where they go"), pill geometry and active/inactive inks all match — only the track fill differs. Hero eyebrow, 56px/900 balance, 2px signature top edge, 150deg gradient, radius 22 and the tile label/figure typography all match.

Card set is DATA-driven in the build, so the exact card population depends on the CreditPrice table for the workspace in the frame; I verified it against packages/db/prisma/seed.ts and the six visible cards line up exactly with the ascending sort of the 12 seeded rows. If the staging workspace carries agency overrides, re-check on the live surface.

Could not be judged from this frame: everything below y≈800 (cards 7–12 and the trailing footnote paragraph) is occluded by the composer, so the rendered appearance of the SMS / call-minute / enrichment / WhatsApp cards and of the footnote is inferred from source. Also unverified live: the empty state "No prices are set for this workspace yet." (BoldCreditsView.tsx line 455) — the prototype has no such state, so it is a build addition rather than a deviation, but its typography (13px, faint, sitting as a bare grid child) has no prototype reference and should be looked at when a workspace with no prices exists.

Deliberately NOT raised, per the settled list: the hero dropping INCLUDED MONTHLY / BURN / RUNS OUT for USED / ADDED; the allowance bar and "N% of your monthly allowance left" being replaced by "Your plan does not carry a monthly credit allowance yet, so there is no share of one to show."; the runway sentence being replaced by the ≥14-days-of-history gate copy; and the Top up button being forest solid with the 34%-white hairline instead of the prototype's gradient "Buy credits" (I treated the label change to "Top up" as riding with that ruling).

Left-rail differences (workspace name, campaign names and counts, "3 elsewhere" vs "1 elsewhere", the Ada-suggests item, the Business core facts/gaps counts, the "$6.4k / $4.1k" campaign values, the collapse "«" affordance, the composer placeholder "Ask about this campaign…" vs "Ask about your setup…") are seed-data and other-surface chrome, not this surface's fidelity, so they are excluded from the rows.

Nothing is left empty to the right of the last card. | BoldCreditsView.tsx line 453: same grid but `minmax(220px, 1fr)` AND `maxWidth: 900`. Measured x=313→1213 (900px) → 3 columns of 291px, leaving a 90px empty gutter between the last card and the content column's right edge (1303), which the hero and the tab strip both still reach. Note the 220 min track alone would still have yielded 4 columns at 990px — the `maxWidth: 900` is what breaks it. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Prototype is truth: drop `maxWidth: 900` and restore the 196px min track so the grid is 4-up at 1440 and terminates flush with the hero.</sub> |

Visible on An email, An email reply, A site-agent turn, A buying signal in the frame. | 🟢 adopt-build<br><sub>honesty-gate — the price is real but no debit path exists (packages/core/src/workspace-settings.ts line 196 pins METERED_CREDIT_ACTIONS to ["lead_reveal"]); without the pill a customer reads a priced card as "you are being billed this". ## Credits → Top-ups

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-credits-topups-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-credits-topups-1440x900.png`

SOURCES READ: both PNGs; `design_handoff_console_v3/prototypes/Console Bold.dc.html` (markup for the `cr.isBills` branch ~lines 2596–2625; data for `cr.plan` / `cr.autoSub` / `cr.invoices` ~lines 5130–5136); `apps/web/components/bold/BoldCreditsView.tsx` (Top-ups branch ~lines 522–585); `apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx` (`AbsentBecause` line 299, `EYEBROW` line 74); `packages/theme/src/console-bold.css` for token values; `design_handoff_console_v3/SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md` §9 (lines 215–270).

STATE MISMATCH — read every row with this in mind. The prototype frame is a fully-seeded workspace (2,340 credits, 14 days of history, three purchases). The build frame is a workspace with an EMPTY ledger (0 used, 0 added, 0 days of history) and no billing. So some blocks are absent because of the honesty gate for THIS data, not because the build cannot render them. I separated the two everywhere it mattered: the burn/runs-out rows get two rows above (gated state = adopt; history-present state = fix, because the code path renders `null`, not the rows).

THINGS THAT MATCH — checked and NOT raised: no leading icon tiles on either side's Top-ups tab (the tinted 26/31/32px tiles are a "Where they go" / "What things cost" device only); no chevrons on either side; both tabs render inline in the canvas — no modal, no drawer, no step counter on this surface; tab strip is identical (labels, order, `#F2F3F2` track, radius 13, padding 4, 22px top margin, active pill white/ink, inactive faint); the list under the eyebrow is bare rows on the page ground in both, not inside a card; hero eyebrow, 56px/900 balance, 2px signature top edge and the tile-strip metrics (`padding:15px 18px`, mono 9px/.12em label, display 20px value, `rgba(255,255,255,.1)` left hairlines) all match.

VERIFIED-BY-MEASUREMENT (not eyeballed): prototype blocks x 313→1302, build x 313→992; prototype card 1 y 459→661, card 2 y 673→740; build well 1 y 492→577, well 2 y 592→677; build well border sampled at y=492 gives a repeating (228,230,229)/(244,246,245) run = a real dashed stroke; build well fill sampled (244,246,245) vs prototype card fill (252,252,252); hero buttons 190×43 @ x1085 (proto, gradient fill sampled (47,200,56)) vs 200×45 @ x1075 (build, fill sampled (20,107,51) = `#146B33`).

CANNOT BE JUDGED FROM THIS FRAME — needs a live check against a seeded workspace: (a) the build's ledger rows have never been seen populated, so the row-composition row above is read off the source, not the pixels; (b) the auto-top-up toggle's OFF sub-line ("Off — Ada will warn you instead") has no build counterpart to compare; (c) the prototype's toggle animation (`left .2s cubic-bezier(.32,.72,0,1)`) has nothing to diff against; (d) hover states — the prototype's invoice rows carry `style-hover="background:#FBFCFB"`, the build's have no hover rule at all, which I could not confirm from a static frame.

BELONGS TO THE buy-1/2/3 PAIR, NOT THIS ONE, but surfaced by the Top up button on this screen and worth flagging there: the prototype's buy flow is a CENTRED MODAL (480px, `position:absolute;inset:0;background:rgba(10,14,12,.2);align-items:center;justify-content:center`) with the step counter in the EYEBROW and counted to two — `['CHOOSE A PACK · 1 OF 2', 'CONFIRM · 2 OF 2', 'DONE']` — and packs that quote real prices ($40/$90/$180), a `best rate` pill, a per-1,000 rate and "about N days of sending". The build uses the shared right-hand `SettingsDrawer` at 424px with `StepDots step of 3` in the PINNED FOOTER, and packs that carry no price at all. SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md line 265 explicitly says "right drawer", so the container is spec-amend territory; the 2-vs-3 step count, the counter's move from eyebrow to footer, and the dropped prices are separate calls for that pair.

CHROME, SHARED ACROSS THE WHOLE b75 SET — not scored here: the prototype puts a rounded-square "?" help button in the canvas head at top-right (x≈1288, y≈72); the build has no header help control and instead a fixed circular forest "?" FAB bottom-right (`.cvb-help-launcher`, 38px, `right:16px;bottom:16px`). Also seed-data only, not fidelity: workspace name ("Bright Smile" vs a truncated "Dem…"), the campaign list and its figures, "3 elsewhere" vs "1 elsewhere", the rail's Business-core chips (14 facts/2 gaps vs 8 facts/8 gaps), and the Ada bar placeholder ("Ask about this campaign…" vs "Ask about your setup…"). The build's rail also shows a collapse chevron the prototype frame does not — again shell, not this surface.

## Buy credits · step 1

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-buy-1-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-buy-1-1440x900.png`

THINGS THAT MATCH (checked, deliberately not raised): no leading icon tile on either side — the pack row's leading element is a 16px radio in both, with identical ring/dot/selected colours; no chevron on either side (the rows are radios, not navigations); pack ladder and order identical (2,000 · 5,000 · 10,000, top to bottom); default selection identical (5,000, second row, mint); the 30px/radius-10/1px-#E4E6E5/12px ✕ closer in the header top-right is pixel-identical; selected-state text colours identical (#0E3D22 title, #1D5B34 sub); both close on scrim click and Esc; no `Back` control at step 1 in either (proto `hasBack: bs.step === 1`, build `step > 0`).

WHAT COULD NOT BE SETTLED FROM THE FRAME (read from source instead, worth a live check): the scrim's reach — the build frame's rail looks only faintly greyed, so I confirmed from `bold-settings-kit.tsx` that the drawer portals to `document.body` with a `position:fixed; inset:0` scrim at z62, i.e. rail + dock + Ada bar all dim, where the prototype's `position:absolute; inset:0` scrim dims the canvas only. Also unjudgeable from a still: the entry animations (`bRise .3s cubic-bezier(.32,.72,0,1)` vs `cvb-over .32s var(--cvb-ease)`), row hover (the proto's pack rows have no `style-hover`; neither does the build), and the drawer's `maxWidth: "92%"` clamp, which never engages at 1440.

BUILD STATE WITH NO PROTOTYPE COUNTERPART: `ChoiceRow`'s sub falls back to `"Added to your balance"` when `summary.balance` is null — a null-balance state the prototype has no equivalent of (its `newBalance` always resolves). Not a deviation, but it is unshot.

THE HONESTY GATES ON THIS SURFACE ARE REAL BUT UNSTATED AT THE POINT OF ABSENCE. I ruled price, per-1,000 rate, days-of-sending and the `best rate` chip as adopt-build-with-reason because no money source exists (no pack/product/price model in the Prisma schema; `CreditPrice.credits` is an Int of CREDITS per action, not currency; the only money-shaped fields in the schema are `Agency.stripeCustomerId`, `valueEstCents` on a lead, and `ProviderInvoice.amount`, none of which price a credit pack). But the build simply drops them in silence on step 1 — the stated reason only appears on step 2, in the `AbsentBecause` about the missing card. The spec's own criterion 7 (line 311-312) and §9.3 require absence WITH a stated reason at the field, and the credits hero already does exactly that. Step 1 should carry the same shape: one gated line on or under the pack list saying there is no price because billing is not connected — which would also reclaim part of the 390px of dead space.

THE SPEC IS INTERNALLY IN CONFLICT HERE AND SHOULD BE AMENDED. Line 266 requires the buy flow to render "2,000/$40 · 5,000/$90 · 10,000/$180 with `best rate` on the last, each showing per-1,000 price and 'about N days of sending'" — every one of which §9.3 and criterion 7 forbid, since none has a source. Line 266's parenthetical needs the same treatment §9.1 got on 2026-08-31: keep the pack ladder, replace the prices/rate/days/chip with what renders when there is no money source, and say so. Filing that would turn four of my adopt-build rows into plain spec compliance. Separately, note that §2 line 47 says prices are IBM Plex Mono — the prototype renders them in Schibsted Grotesk 900, so if pack prices ever land the proto is not the authority on their typeface.

SCOPE: this is step 1 only. The build's step 2 (`Confirm what you are adding`, mint well, `PAYING WITH` + `AbsentBecause` where the proto shows a VISA •••• 4242 / Expires 04/29 row with a cyan `Change`) and step 3 (`Nothing was charged.` vs the proto's ✓ tile + 34px new total + "Receipt emailed. Ada has already resumed anything she was pacing.") carry their own deviations and need their own pairs.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Container type / geometry** | Centred modal floating in the canvas: 480px wide (renders ~478 at x568–1046), height hugs content (~498px, y200–698), radius 22 on all four corners, bg flat #FDFEFD, 1px #E4E6E5, uniform 24px padding, `animation:bRise .3s`. The left rail, the workspace card and the right icon dock all stay visible around it. | Full-height right drawer (`SettingsDrawer`, portalled to document.body): 424px wide × the full 900px viewport (x1016–1440), `position:fixed; top/right/bottom:0`, radius `--cvb-r-drawer` 21px on the LEFT corners only and 0 on the right (flush to the viewport edge), bg `--cvb-gradient-panel` (linear-gradient(180deg,#fff,#f7faf8)), split padding 26/26/16 header · 0/26/20 body · 16/26/22 footer. It covers the right icon dock completely. | 🟡 spec-amend<br><sub>SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md line 266 — "**Buy flow** (3 steps, right drawer)"; line 305 criterion 1 — "Every add label in §11 opens the **right-hand drawer** — no inline forms, no modals" (§11 line 295 maps credits → Top up → buy flow); line 18. Recorded as DEC-140, which states outright that it beats the prototype here.</sub> |

## Buy credits · step 2 (confirm)

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-buy-2-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-buy-2-1440x900.png`

Settled items honoured and not raised: the hero's USED/ADDED tiles, \"Nothing has drawn down credits this month\", the forest-solid Top up button, typed guardrail wells, \"Member\", gap attribution.

Things a frame cannot settle, flagged for a live check:
1. Scrim click-to-close and Escape — the build wires both (SettingsDrawer onKey + scrim onClick); the prototype wires `buy.close` on the scrim only. Not judgeable from a still.
2. Hover states — the prototype gives the modal rows no hover; the build's ChoiceRow/PrimaryButton hovers were not exercised in these frames.
3. The build's drawer sits at z-index 62/63 explicitly to clear the help launcher (61) and tour layer (60). The prototype's modal has no such contender. Not a deviation, just context for why the z-values differ.
4. Step 3 behaviour differs materially (prototype: a ✓ tile, new total and \"Receipt emailed. Ada has already resumed anything she was pacing.\"; build: \"Nothing was charged.\" plus a toast). That belongs to the buy-3 pair, not this one, but it is the reason row 16's verdict is adopt-build-with-reason rather than fix — the two steps have to be judged together.

Out-of-scope differences visible behind the overlay, deliberately NOT filed as rows here because they belong to the credits-usage pair: the page title reads \"Credits and billing\" in the prototype vs \"Credits and usage\" in the build; the hero copy, tiles and the gradient \"Buy credits\" pill differ; the seed workspace differs (Bright Smile Dental vs Demo/Dental Austin), so all figures behind the scrim differ. The one number this surface shares with the hero — balance 2,340 → \"Takes you to 7,340\" — matches in both frames.

Ownership call needed on rows 1 and 3: the container (modal vs drawer) and the step denominator are the only two rows where the build is following a written spec sentence rather than drifting. Resolving them one way collapses rows 2, 14 and 15 (empty space, footer chrome, footer counter) too, so settle those first before dispatching the small-metric fixes.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Container type — the whole surface** | A centred modal. `<sc-if value="{{ buyOpen }}">` renders a scrim `position:absolute;inset:0` with `display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center`, holding a `width:480px` card, `background:#FDFEFD`, `1px solid #E4E6E5` on all four sides, `border-radius:22px`, `padding:24px`, `animation:bRise .3s` (rises from below, centred). ## Buy credits · step 3 (done)

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-buy-3-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-buy-3-1440x900.png`

Sources read in full: prototype markup Console Bold.dc.html:2615-2691 (the buy modal) and model Console Bold.dc.html:5088-5157 (`cr` / `buy`), toast at 3221-3225; build BoldCreditsView.tsx:586-702 (`BuyCreditsDrawer`) and bold-settings-kit.tsx:74-79 (EYEBROW), 257-296 (PrimaryButton), 322-424 (SettingsDrawer), 434-445 (StepPrompt), 467-473 (StepDots). Pixel measurements taken off both PNGs.

Not raised, as instructed (settled): the hero's USED/ADDED tiles replacing INCLUDED MONTHLY / BURN / RUNS OUT, "Nothing has drawn down credits this month", the forest Top up button, roles, gap attribution.

Things that MATCH and are therefore not rows: the close button is identical in both (30x30, radius 10, 1px #E4E6E5 / var(--cvb-line-ctl), ✕ at 12px in #5A6660, top-right, `flex:none`); both close on scrim click and neither surface renders any list rows, so there is no chevron question and no leading-icon question on the done step beyond the missing success tile; neither shows a date; neither shows a price on step 3 (the prototype shows price only on steps 1-2); neither carries a pill or chip on this step (the `best rate` pill lives on step 1 only); neither shows a stat strip.

Could not be judged from the frame alone and would need a live check: (a) whether the build's drawer body scrolls on a short viewport — it is `overflow-y:auto` but the step-3 content is far too short to test; (b) the exact rendered value of `var(--cvb-shadow-two-layer)` on the drawer versus the prototype card's flat 1px border, since the drawer's edge shadow falls off screen-right; (c) the `cvb-over` versus `bRise` animation curves, which a still frame cannot show; (d) whether `flash` on the build's Done click renders in the same pill treatment as the prototype's toast — the drawer closes at the same moment, so it is not in the frame.

One behavioural note worth flagging beyond the visual diff: the build's `BuyCreditsDrawer` never calls any write — steps 1 and 2 only advance local `useState`, and Done just flashes and closes. The prototype's step 1→2 mutates `bought`. This is the mechanism behind rows 8, 9, 15 and 16 and is consistent with the honesty gate, but it means the "Continue" on step 2 (labelled `Pay $N` in the prototype) is a no-op the user may read as a purchase — worth confirming the step-2 copy makes that plain before the user reaches step 3.
