## Add-fact drawer

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-drawer-fact-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-drawer-fact-1440x900.png`

Sources read: prototype `design_handoff_console_v3/prototypes/Console Bold.dc.html` — `ADDFLOW.addfact` (:4881-4884), `addDrawer` (:4886-4918), the `over.isAdd` markup (:1987-2038) and the overlay shell (:1595-1597); build — `apps/web/components/bold/settings/BoldCoreDrawers.tsx` (AddFactDrawer, :50-144) and `apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx` (SURFACE/Well/PrimaryButton/SettingsDrawer/StepPrompt/StepDots/DrawerError); tokens confirmed in `packages/theme/src/console-bold.css`.

Things I checked that MATCH and deliberately did not list: no leading icon tile in either header (the prototype's icon tile belongs to `over.isSender`/`over.isStep`, not `over.isAdd` — the add drawer's header is text-only, and the build agrees); no chevrons in either; no pills, stat strip, price, rate or date anywhere in either drawer; step count is 2 in both; step-1 prompt string and `THE QUESTION` label string are identical; the placeholder "Do you take my insurance?" is identical; final-step label "Teach her" is identical; container is a right-hand drawer in both (not a modal or inline form).

Could not be judged from the frame, needs a live check:
1. The two frames run different seed data (prototype: Bright Smile, 14 facts / 2 gaps, `$2,400 including the crown`; build: 8 facts / 8 gaps, different fact set). Nothing in the drawer diff depends on this, but a like-for-like re-shoot on the same tenant would make the header-wrap and empty-space rows easier for the owner to see.
2. The build frame's BACKGROUND (the Business core item page, not this drawer) leaks a raw cuid `cmth1x47v00067d5z8t9sgcap` as visible page text at y≈748. That belongs to the core item page surface, not the add-fact drawer, so I have not filed it as a row here — but it should be raised against that surface.
3. Prototype scroll behaviour: the whole 392px panel is one `overflow-y:auto` column, so on a short viewport the header scrolls away with the content; the build freezes header and footer and scrolls only the body. On this 1440×900 frame neither scrolls, so the difference is invisible here — I folded it into the pinned-footer row rather than filing it separately.
4. Additive build behaviours with no prototype counterpart and no visual footprint in the frame: Escape-to-close, `role="dialog"` + `aria-label`, `autoFocus` on the well, Enter-to-advance on step 1, and separate question/answer state (the prototype blanks `addVal` between steps, so its step-2 Back loses the typed question). These are improvements, not deviations, and are not filed as rows.
5. Row 12 (recessed well) and rows 14-15 (drawer surface, scrim) are marked spec-amend because SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md §2 overrides the prototype's older atoms console-wide. If the owner wants the prototype's flat-white field and square-cornered drawer back, that is a spec edit at lines 36-38 and 43, not a code fix — it would ripple to every settings drawer, so do not change it in this pass alone.

## Answer-a-gap drawer

> ⚠️ **No frame pair.** `build-drawer-answer-1440x900.png` has no `proto-` counterpart — diffed live against the prototype source, which is cited per row.

OUTCOME IS (A), MISSING PAIR — stated plainly: the prototype has this surface. `Console Bold.dc.html` L4971 sends both `itTab === 'facts'` and `itTab === 'gaps'` to `this.setState({ over: { t: 'addfact' } })`, with no gap payload, so the gaps tab opens `ADDFLOW.addfact.def` — the exact drawer already captured as proto-drawer-fact-1440x900.png. The prototype counterpart for build-drawer-answer is therefore that existing frame; no new prototype shot is needed, and the capture set is not wrong so much as it never had a second thing to shoot. The one real asymmetry is that the prototype has the DRAWER but no gap-aware VARIANT of it, which is why two rows (pre-filled value, help sub-line) resolve as adopt-build-with-reason rather than fix — the build is filling a hole the prototype left, and both fills are already written into SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md §1 L15 / §11 L288 and DEC-141.

Things checked and found MATCHING (deliberately not listed as rows): leading icons — neither side has one on this step (no choice rows until step 2 of other flows, and this flow has none at all); chevrons — none on either side; price / rate / date — neither side shows one, correctly, since a taught fact carries no money or time; field label `THE QUESTION` — same words, same mono 9.5px treatment, same position above the input; step count — genuinely 2 on both sides; close affordance — an `✕` in a hairline rounded square, top-right of the header, on both.

Two sub-pixel deltas judged too small to spend a row on but worth knowing: the close control is 32×32 r11 in the prototype and 30×30 r10 in the build; the drawer title is 20px in the prototype and 22px in the build. Both are shared-kit and would ride any fix to the header block.

Cross-cutting scope warning for the parent: rows 1, 2, 3, 5 and 7 (step indicator, button placement, dead space, prompt typography, eyebrow slot) are properties of the shared `bold-settings-kit.tsx` primitives, not of this surface. They render identically in build-drawer-fact, build-drawer-field, build-drawer-source, build-drawer-addsender, build-drawer-addnumber, build-drawer-invite-1/2 and the three buy frames. If those pairs are judged separately, these will duplicate — fix them once in `SettingsDrawer` / `StepDots` / `StepPrompt` / `PrimaryButton` and every drawer in the set moves together. Rows 6, 8, 9 are the only ones genuinely specific to the answer-a-gap variant.

Not judgeable from a single static frame, needs a live check: (a) step 2 of this flow — the Back button's placement and whether `Teach her` also becomes a right-aligned pill, since only step 1 was captured; (b) whether the pre-filled question is editable (the code says yes, `setQuestion`, but the frame cannot show it) and whether editing it away still closes the gap via the retained `gap.key` — BoldCoreDrawers.tsx L64 carries the key independently of the typed text, so a user who rewrites the question closes a gap whose wording no longer matches, which is a behaviour worth a deliberate ruling rather than an accident; (c) the empty-campaigns case — L110 degrades to a bare `Your live campaigns keep needing this one.` when `gap.campaigns` is empty, which reads oddly and no frame covers it.

Finally, one bookkeeping observation: PROGRESS.md's b75 fidelity row (L485) accounts for 17 pairs and 5 flagged build-only frames, and build-drawer-answer appears in neither tally — it is an uncounted 48th frame. Whichever way this judgement lands, that row should gain a line for it, as a pair against proto-drawer-fact with the gap-variant caveat.

No numeric counter anywhere. | No bars at all. The step position is a mono text counter `1 OF 2` parked in the pinned bottom footer, immediately left of the button (`StepDots`, bold-settings-kit.tsx L467–474; rendered from BoldCoreDrawers.tsx L87). | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Cross-cutting: the same substitution renders in every b75 drawer frame, so dedupe against the add-fact / add-source / add-sender pairs. The build's own component is still named `StepDots` while emitting text, which reads as the bars having been dropped rather than deliberately replaced.</sub> |

| **Header eyebrow** | `NEW FACT` — the prototype's eyebrow convention across the whole ADDFLOW family is a noun naming the artefact being created: `NEW FACT` / `NEW EMAIL SENDER` / `NEW NUMBER` / `NEW KNOWLEDGE SOURCE` (L4866–4884). The gaps path shows `NEW FACT` unchanged, because answering a gap creates a fact. | `ANSWER A GAP` — a verb phrase (BoldCoreDrawers.tsx L76). The build has systematically moved the prototype's TITLE into the eyebrow slot and the tab's add-label into the title, so the eyebrow no longer names the artefact. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Cross-cutting: the non-gap variant shows `TEACH HER SOMETHING` in the same slot, so dedupe against the add-fact pair. The eyebrow is the noun slot in this system; the gap-specific wording belongs in the title, which the next row keeps.</sub> |
| **Drawer title** | `Teach her something` — one title for both entry points; SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md says twice that the gap path opens "the same drawer, pre-filled". | `Fill the gap` — an entry-point-specific title (BoldCoreDrawers.tsx L77). | 🟡 spec-amend<br><sub>design_handoff_console_v3/SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md §1 L15 ("Gap row → same drawer, pre-filled with the asked question") and §11 L288 ("add-fact, pre-filled"). The build's title is the better one — it fits the prototype's short-imperative title convention and matches §11's own add-label "Answer a gap now", where a drawer headed "Teach her something" would read as the wrong drawer — but no spec or DEC records the per-entry-point title, so those two lines should be amended to say the gap path re-titles the drawer.</sub> |
| **THE QUESTION field value** | Empty. `addVal` is `''` and the field shows only the placeholder `Do you take my insurance?` (L4900, L2006); the prototype passes no gap context on the gaps→add route (L4971), so the clicked gap is lost. | Pre-filled with the gap's own question, rendered in ink: `What makes you different` (`useState(gap?.label ?? '')`, BoldCoreDrawers.tsx L52), with `gap.key` carried through to the write (L64). | 🟢 adopt-build<br><sub>prototype-defect — the prototype's gaps→add loses which gap you meant, so the user retypes it and creates a near-duplicate fact beside a gap that never closes. The 424 vs 392 width is the one part of this row nothing documents, but it follows coherently from the container change.</sub> |

## Add-field drawer

> ⚠️ **No frame pair.** `build-drawer-field-1440x900.png` has no `proto-` counterpart — diffed live against the prototype source, which is cited per row.

OUTCOME (B) — the prototype genuinely has no add-field drawer; this is not a missing pair.

Evidence, from `design_handoff_console_v3/prototypes/Console Bold.dc.html`:
- :4965 the `who`/fields tab does get an add label — `addL: { fields: 'Add a field', … }` — and :4964 `hasAdd` includes `fields`, so the dashed-plus affordance behind the drawer is real and matches the build's page.
- :4968-4972 `add:` routes `email`/`num` → `{t:'addsender'}`, `sources` → `{t:'addsource'}`, `facts`/`gaps` → `{t:'addfact'}`, and then falls through to `this.flash(itTab === 'fields' ? 'New field' : 'New answer')` — a toast, no overlay state, nothing to capture.
- :4877-4889 `ADDFLOW` contains exactly four flows: `addsender.email`, `addsender.num`, `addsource.def`, `addfact.def`. There is no field (or answer) flow, so `addDrawer` can never be built for it. Consistent with the frame set: `proto-drawer-field` and `proto-drawer-answer` do not exist, while their build twins do.

The build addition is authorised, and its copy is exact. SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md:122-124 specifies "Add-field drawer (2 steps, new)" with the prompts "Name the thing. She quotes it exactly as you write it, and never guesses around it." → "What goes in it?", labels `THE FIELD`/`THE VALUE`, completion `Save the field`; §11 line 289 maps core/who → "Add a field" → add-field. `AddFieldDrawer` (apps/web/components/bold/settings/BoldCoreDrawers.tsx:149-212) matches all of that verbatim. Header naming also follows the prototype's own convention (`label` = NEW <NOUN> → "NEW FIELD"; `name` = the instruction → "Add a field", the same shape as "Add an email sender"/"Add a number") — note the build's *add-fact* drawer inverts that convention ("TEACH HER SOMETHING" / "Add something she should know"), so on the header this build-only surface is the more faithful of the two. No row.

Fix once, not seven times. Rows 1-4 all live in the shared kit (`apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx` — `SettingsDrawer` footer slot, `StepDots`, `StepPrompt`, `PrimaryButton`), so the same four deviations will read identically on add-fact, add-answer, add-source, add-sender, add-number, invite and the buy flow. They should be settled in the kit; this frame is just where they are most exposed, because a single short input above 598px of empty panel makes the pinned footer and the missing step bars impossible to miss.

Not judgeable from this frame — needs a live check:
- Step 2 is uncaptured: `THE VALUE`, a 4-row multiline well, the Back pill appearing, the counter reading "2 OF 2", and the "Save the field" primary. Only step 1 was shot; the §8 state matrix for a 2-step drawer should carry both.
- Error state (`DrawerError`) and the busy "Working…" swap on the primary are uncaptured.
- Not applicable here, so deliberately not filed as rows on this surface: leading icons (neither the prototype's `addDrawer` header nor the build's has one — icons appear only on the sender/step drawers), chevrons (no rows in this drawer), and any price/rate/date (neither shows one; correct, an add-field step has no money in it). The choice-row shape is likewise not exercised — but flagging it for the add-source/add-sender review: the prototype's `addOpts` row (Console Bold.dc.html:2011-2021) is a 28px rounded-square icon tile + title + sub with a right-hand ✓ when selected, whereas the build's `ChoiceRow` (bold-settings-kit.tsx:144-200) uses a left 16px radio dot and no tile and no ✓. That is a real deviation on those surfaces, not this one.
- Trivial atom drift, inside canon and left off the rows: drawer title 22px/-.032em vs the prototype's 20px/-.03em (SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS §2 permits 18-22px card titles), and eyebrow tracking .18em vs .16em.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **step indicator for the 2-step flow** | Two full-width segment bars sit directly under the drawer title (`addDots`, Console Bold.dc.html:1995-1999 + 4898): flex:1 each, height 3px, gap 4px, radius 2px — done #146B33, current #CFE8D8, remaining #EDEEED. Visible in proto-drawer-fact-1440x900.png as a mint segment beside a grey one at y≈120. | No bars anywhere. The step state is a mono text counter "1 OF 2" (9.5px, .16em, faint) parked in the bottom footer to the left of the Next pill. `StepDots` in apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx:467-473 is named for dots but renders `{step + 1} OF {of}` text. Nothing under the title — the header runs straight into the prompt. | 🔴 fix |
| **primary action placement (and the ~600px of dead panel it creates)** | Inline, hugging the content: the action row is `margin-top:20px` immediately after the input, the green primary is `flex:1` so it spans the full drawer width, and Back (grey pill) sits to its left only from step 2. No footer bar, no divider rule — the whole ask reads as one block and the empty panel falls below it (Console Bold.dc.html:2032-2037). | A pinned footer bar at the drawer's bottom edge with a 1px top divider (measured at y=818), holding [Back] · spacer · "1 OF 2" · a compact right-aligned ~72px "Next" pill (`SettingsDrawer` footer slot, bold-settings-kit.tsx:413-426; PrimaryButton is `flex:"none"`, padding 12/17). The input well ends at y=220, so 598px — 66% of the drawer's height — of empty panel separates the field from its own action. | 🔴 fix |
| **primary button label on the non-final step** | `addNextL` is literally "Continue" on every non-last step; only the last step earns a specific verb ("Teach her", "Start reading it", "Get the number", "Finish") — Console Bold.dc.html:4913. | "Next" (BoldCoreDrawers.tsx:181). The last step is correct — "Save the field" is SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md:124 verbatim — so only the step-1 label departs. | 🔴 fix |

Fix: split it — prompt "Name the thing.", help "She quotes it exactly as you write it, and never guesses around it." — or render the single line in the prototype's body grey. | 🔴 fix |

Measured at y=173-220 in the frame as a grey filled well, not a white box. | 🟢 adopt-build<br><sub>canon-rule — SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md §2 (lines 36-37): "Inputs are recessed wells, never more white boxes: #F4F6F5 + inset 0 1px 2px rgba(16,22,19,.05) + 1px solid #DFE3E1. The right icon rail stays visible beside it (proto-drawer-fact-1440x900.png: drawer right edge at x≈1343, rail at x≈1388). | 424px, `position:fixed` top/right/bottom, flush to the browser edge at x=1016→1440 and covering the rail; rounded left corners (`var(--cvb-r-drawer) 0 0 var(--cvb-r-drawer)`), panel gradient + two-layer shadow, portalled to document.body at z-index 63 (bold-settings-kit.tsx:360-379). | 🟢 adopt-build<br><sub>canon-rule — SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md §2: scrim is normative as `position:fixed; inset:0` console-wide (line 43-45), which forces a viewport-level surface; radii "20-22 drawers/frames" (line 38) and two-layer elevation "on cards, drawers and sheets" (lines 32-34) give the corners and shadow.</sub> |

## Add-source drawer

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-drawer-source-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-drawer-source-1440x900.png`

Sources read: prototype "Console Bold.dc.html" — ADDFLOW.addsource L4877–4880, addDrawer L4886–4918 (OPTS at L4892–4893, option tint triples at L4903–4905), overlay shell L1596–1597, isAdd markup L1987–2038, overlay ancestor chain (canvas card at L149). Build — apps/web/components/bold/settings/BoldCoreDrawers.tsx L349–510 and apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx (ChoiceRow L144, PrimaryButton L257, SettingsDrawer L322, StepPrompt L434, StepDots L467, Well L83, EYEBROW L72). Tokens from packages/theme/src/console-bold.css.

Things that MATCH and are deliberately not listed: eyebrow text "NEW KNOWLEDGE SOURCE"; the three option titles and their order (A website / A document / Typed by you); selected/unselected row fills and borders (#EAF5EE on #CFE8D8 selected, #FCFCFC on #ECEDEC unselected — the build's --cvb-mint/--cvb-mint-line/--cvb-panel-quiet/--cvb-line resolve to exactly those hexes); primary button radius 12, fontSize 12.5, weight 800, forest fill; Back hidden on step 0; last-step primary label "Start reading it"; close glyph ✕; two steps total; no Ada card and no record line on the add drawer (correct — `ada:''` makes `hasAda` false); no price, rate or date is shown in either build (correct, none belongs here); no chevrons in either build (correct — the option rows are radio/check rows, not navigation rows).

Could not be judged from the frame and needs a live check: hover and focus-visible states on the choice rows and the primary (neither source defines a hover for ChoiceRow; the prototype defines none either, so this may be a shared gap rather than a deviation); the exact rendered width of the build's Next button (measured from padding, not the pixels); whether the build's `--cvb-shadow-lift` on the primary is visible at this scale; whether the document branch's redirect is a permanent product decision or a not-yet-wired upload path — if upload is wired later, rows 20/24 collapse into one fix.

Ordering note for the fix pass: rows 1–3 (icon tiles, trailing ✓, default selection) and rows 7–8 (progress bars in header vs "1 OF 2" in footer) are the deviations that change what the surface IS, not how it is tuned; row 12 (dead vertical space) is downstream of rows 4 and 11 and will partly resolve when the sub-lines shorten and the footer un-pins.

Padding is split three ways on a fixed header/scroll body/fixed footer: '26px 26px 16px' / '0 26px 20px' / '16px 26px 22px'. | 🔴 fix |
| **Step 2 — structure (one step vs three kind-specific branches)** | ONE step 2 for every choice: `steps[1] = ['Paste the address or drop the file. I re-read a website weekly; a file only when you replace it.', 'THE SOURCE', 'https://yoursite.com/prices', 'field']` (L4878–4879). The chosen kind changes nothing about the step's shape. | Three entirely different step-2 bodies keyed on `kind` (BoldCoreDrawers.tsx L426–507): website → prompt + 2 wells; document → a redirect notice + 2 wells; typed → prompt + 2 wells. Three different layouts, three different prompts, three different field sets behind one progress state. | 🔴 fix |
| **Step 2 — field label and placeholder** | Label `THE SOURCE`, placeholder `https://yoursite.com/prices`. Label styling 9.5px mono, letter-spacing .13em, margin-bottom 6. | Label `THE ADDRESS`, placeholder `https://brightsmile.com/pricing`. Well label is 9.5px mono, letterSpacing .14em, marginBottom 8. | 🔴 fix |
| **Step 2 — extra second field** | Exactly one field on the address step. | A second Well, `CALL IT (OPTIONAL)` with placeholder "Pricing page", stacked at marginTop 14 below the address (BoldCoreDrawers.tsx L440–448). The document and typed branches add a `CALL IT` + `WHAT IT SAYS` pair. None of these exist in the prototype. | 🔴 fix |
| **Step 2 — sub-line copy** | One body line, 13px #5A6660: "Paste the address or drop the file. I re-read a website weekly; a file only when you replace it." | Split into StepPrompt heading "Which address?" (15px/700 ink) + help line "I re-read a website weekly; a file only when you replace it." (12.5px, var(--cvb-faint)). The "Paste the address or drop the file" clause — the only thing that tells the user a file can be dropped — is dropped. | 🔴 fix |
| **Document path — file drop vs redirect** | Choosing "A document" leads to the same address-or-file step whose copy explicitly offers "drop the file". | Choosing "A document" replaces step 2 with a refusal: "Uploading a file happens from a campaign's knowledge step." plus help "Documents go through the upload path so the file itself is stored and re-readable. Type the same content here instead and she has it immediately.", then asks for CALL IT + WHAT IT SAYS. There is no file input, and `finish()` routes document through `addTypedSource` — the same call as "Typed by you", so the two options do the same thing. | 🔴 fix |
| **Field styling — input vs recessed well** | Plain input: background #FCFCFC, border 1px #E4E6E5, border-radius 11, padding 12px 13px, font-size 13.5, no inner shadow. | SURFACE.well — background var(--cvb-well-fill-2) #f4f6f5, border 1px var(--cvb-well-line-2) #dfe3e1, borderRadius 14, boxShadow var(--cvb-shadow-well) inset, padding '12px 14px', fontSize 13.5. | 🟡 spec-amend<br><sub>design_handoff_console_v3/SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md lines 36–37: "Inputs are recessed wells, never more white boxes: #F4F6F5 + inset 0 1px 2px rgba(16,22,19,.05) + 1px solid #DFE3E1. This applies to every field in every add drawer."</sub> |
| **Completion toast copy** | `this.flash('Reading started — 9 pages queued')` (L4916) — names the queue depth. | "Reading started — it lands on the sources tab as it goes." for website; "Saved — she is reading it now." for document/typed (BoldCoreDrawers.tsx L370–374). No page count. | 🟢 adopt-build<br><sub>honesty-gate — the page count is not knowable at submit time (it comes out of the crawl), so the build cannot state "9 pages" without inventing it. Note this also departs from SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md line 132, which repeats the prototype's toast.</sub> |

## Add-email-sender drawer

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-drawer-addsender-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-drawer-addsender-1440x900.png`

Method: both PNGs read and pixel-sampled (edge scans and colour probes) to settle geometry, then the prototype's `ADDFLOW.addsender.email` model (line 4867) and its `isAdd` render branch (lines 1987–2039) plus the drawer shell (lines 1595–1597) against the build's `AddEmailSenderDrawer` (apps/web/components/bold/settings/BoldSenderDrawers.tsx) and the kit primitives in apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx.

What MATCHES and was deliberately not raised: the eyebrow text (NEW EMAIL SENDER), the title (Add an email sender), the ✕ close in the top-right, the step COUNT (3 in both) and the step ORDER (address → DNS → warm-up), the final button label (Finish), the absence of a leading icon tile in the drawer header (neither has one), the absence of an ✦ Ada note and of a mono record line (the prototype sets `ada:''`/`rec:''` on add drawers and gates them off), the left border hairline (#ECEDEC both), the entrance animation (both 0.32s on cubic-bezier(.32,.72,0,1); the prototype's `bOver` translates 30px from the right, the build's `cvb-over` uses the same easing token), and the field-label wording THE ADDRESS.

Only step 1 is visible in the frames; steps 2 and 3 were diffed from source, as instructed. If you want frames for those, the build testids are `bold-drawer-addsender-next` (to step 2), `bold-drawer-addsender-dnsstatus`, `bold-dns-block` / `bold-dns-block-empty`, and `bold-drawer-addsender-pace-{careful|standard|fast}`.

Two things I could not judge from the frame and that need a live check:
1. The DNS step's real record set for Bright Smile. `DnsBlock` iterates `['spf','dkim','dmarc']`, so if the seeded sender carries a DMARC row the step will render three records under a prompt that says "two". Worth confirming against the seed before deciding whether the fix is the copy or the record list.
2. `DnsBlock`'s empty state ("No check has run yet, so there is nothing to show. The records appear the moment the first check comes back.") never appears in a frame. It has no prototype counterpart at all and is an honesty gate; it is listed here only as context, not as a deviation row.

Two trivia deliberately kept out of the rows: the completion toast differs by a full stop (prototype `'Sender added — checking DNS'`, build `"Sender added — checking DNS."` — the spec quotes it with the stop at line 161), and the build autofocuses THE ADDRESS while the prototype does not.

Structural note behind several rows: the build creates the sender with `POST /senders` when Next is pressed on step 1, so steps 2 and 3 are post-write. The prototype's flow writes nothing until Finish. That is why Back vanished on the DNS step and it is worth an explicit ruling — it changes what "Finish" means, not just what it looks like.

No row here touches the settled list (guardrail typed wells, the credits hero's USED/ADDED, the no-draw-down sentence, the forest Top up with its 34%-white hairline, "Member" not "Agent", gaps attributed to campaigns) — none of those appear on this surface."

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|

Measured in the frame: drawer occupies x 952–1342, y 24–876; the left sidebar stays bright white (#FCFCFC sampled at x=100) and the right icon dock rail (x≈1370–1405) stays fully visible, bright and outside the drawer. Only the canvas is dimmed (#DCDDDD sampled). | Portalled to `document.body`. Scrim is `position:fixed; inset:0; var(--cvb-scrim)` = rgba(16,22,19,.26) over the WHOLE viewport (z 62) and the panel is `position:fixed; top:0; right:0; bottom:0` (z 63). Measured: drawer occupies x 1016–1440, y 0–900 — flush to all three viewport edges; it covers the right icon dock rail completely (the dock is absent from the build frame) and the left sidebar is dimmed to (191,193,192). | 🟡 spec-amend<br><sub>SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md lines 43–45 ("Every popover, drawer and sheet dims the page behind it: scrim `position:fixed; inset:0; rgba(16,22,19,.26)`"), carried into DEC-140/DEC-147. ## Add-a-number drawer

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-drawer-addnumber-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-drawer-addnumber-1440x900.png`

SETTLED items were checked and excluded: no guardrail caps, credits hero, drawdown copy, Top up button, role wording or gap attribution appear on this surface.

Matches — no row raised: the eyebrow string `NEW NUMBER` and the title `Add a number` are identical and stay constant across all three steps in both; the step count is 3 in both; the step order is identical (area → what it carries → A2P filing); the two option titles `SMS only` / `SMS and voice` match; the default selection is `SMS and voice` in both (prototype `s.addOpt || OPTS[1][3]`, build `useState<"sms"|"sms_voice">("sms_voice")`); neither shows a chevron on any choice row; neither shows a price, monthly rate or a date anywhere in the drawer; neither carries a stat strip or any pill (the build's ChoiceRow supports a `meta` chip but the number flow passes none); neither shows an Ada ✦ note or a mono record line (prototype sets `ada: ''`, so `hasAda` is false and the block is skipped); Back appears only from step 2 onward in both.

Could not be judged from the frame — needs a live check:
1. The build's step-1 `Next` is gated by `/^\d{3}$/.test(areaCode.trim())` and, when the test fails, the onClick simply does nothing — no disabled styling, no `aria-disabled`, no inline error. It looks live and is inert. The prototype's Continue always advances. This is a real deviation but it has no visual signature in a single frame; worth confirming in the running app and either disabling the button visibly or showing why it will not advance.
2. The build renders a `DrawerError` slot on step 3 (BoldSenderDrawers.tsx, after the amber card) for a failed `requestNumber` POST. The prototype has no error surface at all. This is a robustness addition rather than a fidelity deviation, so no row — but its styling (`--cvb-danger-bg`, `1px solid #ecd2cb`, radius 13) has no prototype counterpart to check against.
3. On the step-3 honesty row: only the false clause is gated. Ada's first-person voice ("I file it…") can be restored honestly — e.g. an Ada-voiced sentence that states the filing is not connected — and the prototype's dark mono record block could be kept for the two facts that ARE true (the area code and what it carries) with the false `status filed` line dropped. Recommend that rather than treating the amber card as settled.
4. The two frames sit on different underlying data (prototype: 1 existing number, warm-up 82%, `Austin, TX (512)`; build: 0 numbers, warm-up 20%, empty area field). That is a fixture difference, not a drawer deviation, so no row — but it means the build frame never exercises the drawer's populated state, and the mono caution line on the Numbers tab behind the drawer ("RESERVING A NUMBER AND FILING A2P WITH THE CARRIERS IS NOT CONNECTED YET…") plus the `cmth1x47v00067d5z8t9sgcap` id line under it are page-level, out of this surface's scope.
5. Spec lines to amend if these verdicts are accepted: design_handoff_console_v3/SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md L163 (restore \"Pick where the number should look local.\"), L164–165 (drop \"I file it with your business details — nothing for you to do\" and \"Get the number\"), L166 (the completion toast). L32–38 and L43–45 stand as written but now knowingly override the prototype for drawer skin, well fill and scrim.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Drawer width and how far it reaches — container scope** | 392px wide (max-width 88%), rendered as `position:absolute; inset:0` INSIDE the console shell. Measured in the frame: left hairline x=951, right edge x=1344. ## Sender drawer

`docs/fidelity/b75/proto-drawer-sender-1440x900.png` vs `docs/fidelity/b75/build-drawer-sender-1440x900.png`

Frames are of different sender records — the prototype frame shows the non-warming `hello@brightsmile.com` (SENT·30D 3,910 / BOUNCES 0.4% / COMPLAINTS 0, no warm-up block, all three DNS records passing, ✦ note "Your main domain, clean for eleven months"), the build frame shows a zeroed, unchecked, warming `team@brightsmile.test`. I diffed structure, not values, and read the prototype's `senderDrawer(id)` view-model (Console Bold.dc.html:4841-4877) plus the `over.isSender` render (:1932-1988) and the shared `hasAda`/`rec` footer (:2040-2048) so the warming and non-warming variants are both covered.

Things I could NOT settle from the frame or the source and that want a live check:
· Whether the 7-day stat window is a real backend constraint or just a default — `health?.windowDays ?? 7` implies the API supplies it, so a 30-day read may already be available; if it is, the eyebrow should go back to `SENT · 30D`.
· Whether the "Four more days at this pace" warm-up projection has a data source. If it genuinely does not, the drawer still needs to SAY that (the credits precedent is drop-plus-stated-reason), not drop the sentence silently — as written it just ends early.
· Whether the build ever renders a warm-up block in the non-warming case: the build gates on `warm?.active`, the prototype on a hard-coded `warm` flag, so both hide it — no deviation, noted only so it is not read as one.
· The inline cap editor (action row swaps in place for a `SENDS A DAY` well + Save) has no prototype counterpart — the prototype's "Change the daily cap" is a stub that only flashes "Cap editor". I did not raise it as a row because there is nothing to diff against, and the typed-well form is the settled owner ruling. It is worth an owner look as new behaviour.
· The build drawer's `DrawerError` slot has no prototype counterpart either; same reasoning.

Not raised as rows because they belong to the Senders ITEM PAGE surface, not this drawer, but they are visible in both frames and are real: the prototype's sender list rows carry a mint ✉ tile per row and the build's carry none; the meta strip reads `EMAIL DOMAINS 2 both authenticated · NUMBERS 1 local Austin area code · WARM-UP 82% full volume in 4 days` in the prototype vs `EMAIL SENDERS 2 0 of 2 authenticated · NUMBERS 0 none yet · WARM-UP 20% still ramping` in the build; and the build adds an all-caps line "ONE IDENTITY PER WORKSPACE PER CHANNEL — CAMPAIGNS RESOLVE IT AUTOMATICALLY, NO PICKERS." that the prototype does not have. Route those to the Senders item-page pass.

On empty space: the build drawer is not under-filled — its content runs to y≈752 vs the prototype's y≈682. The problem is the opposite of a modal-in-a-drawer: the build spends its extra height on looser gaps, oversized stat values and three added sub-lines while dropping the ✦ note that the prototype uses to close the panel.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Header leading identity tile** | 42×42 tile, border-radius 14, bg #EAF5EE / border #CFE8D8 / glyph #146B33, glyph ✉ at 16px, sitting left of the eyebrow+name column with gap 12 (number variant: glyph ✆ on the cyan triple #E2F3F6 / #BFE3EB / #0E7D93). | No tile at all. `SettingsDrawer` (bold-settings-kit.tsx:322) has no icon slot — the eyebrow and title start flush at the 26px left padding. The green/cyan channel cue that tells email from number at a glance is gone. | 🔴 fix |
| **Drawer title typography** | The address is a label, not a display heading: font-weight 800, font-size 16px, letter-spacing -.024em, margin-top 5px, `word-break:break-all` so a long address wraps inside the 392px column. | `cvb-display` class, font-weight 900, font-size 22px, letter-spacing -.032em, margin-top 8, line-height 1.15 (bold-settings-kit.tsx:383-389). `team@brightsmile.test` renders at display scale and dominates the drawer. | 🔴 fix |
| **Stat strip container** | The three stats live inside ONE quiet card: `display:flex; background:#FCFCFC; border:1px solid #ECEDEC; border-radius:15px; overflow:hidden; margin-top:18px`, each cell padding 13px 12px, separated by a `border-left:1px solid #F1F2F1` (transparent on the first cell). The stats sit directly on the drawer ground. | 🔴 fix |

## Invite drawer · step 1

> ⚠️ **No frame pair.** `build-drawer-invite-1-1440x900.png` has no `proto-` counterpart — diffed live against the prototype source, which is cited per row.

OUTCOME (B) — the prototype genuinely has no invite drawer. This is NOT a missing pair; there is nothing to capture.

Evidence: `people` is in `hasAdd` and gets the add label 'Invite someone' (Console Bold.dc.html:4966–4967), but the `add()` handler (4968–4972) routes only email/num/sources/facts/gaps into an `over` drawer and falls through to `this.flash(...)` for people — and that fall-through's ternary only knows 'fields' and 'answers', so tapping Invite someone in the prototype flashes the wrong toast, 'New answer'. There is no `people` entry in ADDFLOW (4865–4884) and no invite branch at the overlay switch (5985). SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md:20 says the same in its own words ("Invite someone absent (also absent from the proto)"), and §7:184 calls the invite drawer "2 steps, new — this was missing from the proto too". The spec's claim that it is "now designed and in the proto (§7)" is not true of the .dc.html at hand — the design lives in the spec prose, not the prototype.

WHAT THE BUILD GETS RIGHT against the convention (not rows): right-hand drawer over a scrim; mono eyebrow + heavy display title + bordered ✕ top-right; mono field label over a recessed full-width input; Back correctly suppressed on step 1; two steps for a two-decision flow (same count as addfact); no chevrons and no leading icons, which matches — the prototype's field steps carry neither; and no price/rate/date, which also matches — no prototype add drawer shows one at a field step.

NOT JUDGEABLE FROM THIS FRAME: the step-2 choice rows. For the record they are radio-dot rows (bold-settings-kit.tsx:144–207) where the prototype's `addOpts` rows carry a 28×28 bordered leading icon tile plus a trailing ✓ (2011–2018) — but the prototype's rows are icon-bearing *kind* pickers and roles have no glyph in the PROGRESS.md icon map, so the radio shape is plausibly correct. Judge it on build-drawer-invite-2, where the roles render Admin / Member / Viewer per SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md:185–187 (the code's internal `AGENT` maps to the word "Member" via `workspaceRoleWord`).

ONE OWNER CALL, deliberately not filed as a row: the prototype's add drawers state the consequential fact inside the step prompt ("I re-read a website weekly", "A2P registration takes about a day", "40 a day, 3 weeks to full volume"). The invite's seven-day expiry appears only in the post-send toast (BoldTeamItem.tsx:256) and is never said in the drawer. That belongs to step 2, not this frame; if it should be stated before you commit, it is a spec-amend at SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS.md:184–188.

SCOPE OF THE FIX: rows 1–5 and 7 all live in the shared `apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx`, not in the invite drawer — one edit, not six, and it moves every B7.5 drawer at once (fact, source, sender, number, buy). `build-drawer-fact-1440x900.png` shows the identical missing bar, `1 OF 2` counter, right-flushed pill and ~580px dead band against a `proto-drawer-fact` that HAS the segment bar and the inline full-width `Continue`, so verify there before landing — that captured pair may already carry these findings.

NOT CHECKABLE FROM A STILL: the email input's autoFocus, Esc-to-close, and Enter-to-advance on step 1 are all wired in the source but need a live run to confirm.

No numeric step counter exists anywhere in any prototype drawer. | No bar at all. Progress is a mono `1 OF 2` counter parked in the bottom footer beside the button (`StepDots`, bold-settings-kit.tsx:467, used at BoldTeamItem.tsx:271). The header runs eyebrow → title → straight into the prompt with nothing between. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>canon-rule — UI_PORTING_RULES: tokens govern atoms, the prototype governs composition, and it names drawers as composition. No DEC-140..149 rules drawer chrome, so nothing overrides the prototype here.</sub> |

| **Vertical dead space in the drawer body** | The drawer is top-packed and ends where its content ends — a two-element step closes right below the button, with no reserved bottom band. | Content stops at the email input (bottom ≈y250); the footer rule sits at y≈830. Roughly 580px — about 64% of the drawer's height — is empty, and the only thing at the bottom of a 900px-tall panel is a small pill. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Same root cause as the pinned-footer row; unpinning the footer resolves both. (`StepPrompt`, bold-settings-kit.tsx:434–442). Titles fit one line at 392px. | Inverted: the eyebrow is the add-row's imperative `INVITE SOMEONE` and the title is a different sentence, "Add someone to this workspace", which wraps to two lines even at the wider 424px (BoldTeamItem.tsx:267–268). Under the convention this reads eyebrow `NEW TEAMMATE` + title "Invite someone". | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Build-wide, not invite-only: build-drawer-fact makes the identical swap (`TEACH HER SOMETHING` / "Add something she should know" against the proto's `NEW FACT` / "Teach her something"), and that one IS a captured pair — count it once, not twice.</sub> |

## Pending-invite drawer

> ⚠️ **No frame pair.** `build-drawer-invite-2-1440x900.png` has no `proto-` counterpart — diffed live against the prototype source, which is cited per row.

OUTCOME IS (B), and it is confirmed twice over: by the prototype source (the `people` add-row at Console Bold.dc.html:4967 flashes rather than opening a drawer; `ADDFLOW` at :4863 has only addsender/addsource/addfact) and by the repo's own fidelity log (PROGRESS.md line 485 lists the invite drawer at both steps among "5 build-only frames, flagged"). This is not a missing capture.

THE CENTRAL FINDING, and where the fix belongs: rows 2, 3, 4 and 8 are all one defect in one shared atom — `ChoiceRow` in /home/user/clientforce-platform/apps/web/components/bold/bold-settings-kit.tsx:144 was written as a radio group where the prototype's choice step is an icon-tile list with a trailing tick. That matters beyond this build-only frame, because the SAME `ChoiceRow` is used by `AddSourceDrawer` (/home/user/clientforce-platform/apps/web/components/bold/settings/BoldCoreDrawers.tsx:405–425), which DOES have a prototype counterpart — `◍ A website` / `◫ A document` / `✎ Typed by you`, with icon tiles and a `✓`. So a paired surface already ships this divergence and the pair may not have caught it. Fix `ChoiceRow` once (add an optional leading glyph tile, move the selected mark to a trailing `✓`, hold title/sub colours constant) and both surfaces land together. Same for rows 1, 5, 6 and 7: they live in `SettingsDrawer`/`StepPrompt`/`StepDots` in the same kit file and affect every settings drawer in this family, not just this one.

DELIBERATELY NOT REPORTED as rows, being drawer chrome that the invite-1 judgment covers on the same shell: panel width 424 vs the prototype's 392; the left-corner radius (`--cvb-r-drawer`) and gradient panel fill vs the prototype's square-cornered flat `#FFFFFF`; title 22px vs 20px (which is why "Add someone to this workspace" wraps to two lines here and no prototype drawer title does); close button 30×30/r10 vs 32×32/r11. Flagging them again per-step would double-count. One step-1-only item also out of scope for this frame: the build's non-final primary reads "Next" where the prototype's convention is "Continue" (line 4913).

COULD NOT BE JUDGED FROM A STATIC FRAME — needs a live check: hover and focus treatment on the choice rows (the prototype gives them only `cursor:pointer`, no hover state, so the build must not invent one); keyboard behaviour of a `role="radio"` set that has no `radiogroup` wrapper and no arrow-key handling; and the error state — `DrawerError` (bold-settings-kit.tsx:446) renders under the options on a failed send and has no captured frame in docs/fidelity/b75/, so its spacing against the footnote and the footer is unverified. Also unverifiable statically: whether the ~370px of dead space collapses at shorter viewports or simply grows.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |

No numeric counter anywhere in the add drawer. | No progress bars at all, at either step. Instead a mono `2 OF 2` counter sits in the pinned footer between the Back pill and the primary (`StepDots`, bold-settings-kit.tsx:467), 8px from the button and visually cramped against its shadow. | 🔴 fix |
| **Choice-row leading icon** | Every `opts` row opens with a 28×28 rounded icon tile (`border-radius:10`, bg `#fff` selected / `#F2F3F2` not, border `#CFE8D8`/`#E4E6E5`, glyph `#146B33`/`#5A6660` at 12px) carrying an abstract glyph — `◷ ◈ ⌃` for warm-up pace, `◍ ◫ ✎` for source kind, `✆ ☎` for number kind (lines 4894, 2013). ## Person drawer

> ⚠️ **No frame pair.** `build-drawer-person-1440x900.png` has no `proto-` counterpart — diffed live against the prototype source, which is cited per row.

OUTCOME (B) — the prototype genuinely has no counterpart for this surface. Three independent confirmations:

1. The prototype DOES have an `over.t === 'person'` drawer, but it is the CONTACT drawer, not a team drawer: built by `person(k)` from `PEOPLE_DB` (Console Bold.dc.html ~L3663), rendering avatar + quick-action tiles + pipeline value/touches + TAGS + NOTES + TIMELINE + `contact ct_7781` — Grace O'Brien, Daniel Okoro, Nina Petrova. It has no roles, no scopes, no remove.
2. It is only reachable from inbox response rows: `mkRows('resp')` is the sole caller of `over: { t: 'person', k: r.p }` (~L4712). The `ws:team` People rows (`ITEMS['ws:team']`, L4541-4548) fall through mkRows' generic branch, where `SETOPTS['Godswill Eze']` is undefined, so those rows get `cur:'default'` and `go: () => {}` — clicking a team person in the prototype opens nothing. Its "Invite someone" add button likewise only `flash()`es.
3. SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS §7 specifies only the invite drawer for this page, and its §17 fidelity-frame list names "invite drawer" but no person/role drawer. It does require the states this drawer exists to serve — "a person's role changed", "removing a person, with the honest consequence line", "the last owner cannot be removed or demoted" — so the drawer is a legitimate build invention filling a designed-in gap, not drift. Nothing here warrants a spec-amend; the spec never claimed a shape for it.

Credited, not flagged: the `ON YOUR TEAM` eyebrow (the prototype's human drawers have no eyebrow, but every settings-family drawer does — the build picked the right family); the amber last-owner rail and the two-stage remove confirm (both spec §7 states, and neither is inventable from the prototype); the join date being real `member.since` data.

Not judged from the frame, needs a live check:
- The last-owner state is NOT in this frame (this workspace has two owners), so the amber block, the frozen ring, and the suppressed remove link are unverified visually — that state needs its own capture for the §8 matrix.
- Drawer width is 424px in the build vs 392px in the prototype's `overOpen`, and the close button is 30px/r10 vs 32px/r11. Both are kit-wide constants shared with the PAIRED sender and add-sender drawers, so they belong on those pairs rather than here; I have not raised them as rows.
- `StepDots` renders a text counter ("1 OF 2") where the prototype's `addDots` are segmented progress bars (`flex:1;height:3px`). Not applicable to this drawer (single pane, correctly no step counter) — flagging it only so it is not lost; it belongs to the invite and add-sender pairs.
- No price/rate is shown here and none should be; the only number is the join date, handled in the sub-line row.
- The prototype fills the region the build leaves empty with evidence (sender: 3-up stat strip + AUTHENTICATION block; person: value/touches tiles + TIMELINE). I have not asked for invented evidence here — the record line alone is the honest fill — but if any of "last active", "role changed by X on Y", or "N conversations assigned" is already queryable, that region is where the prototype would put it.

| Element | Prototype | Build | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Primary action placement + the gap above it ("Save the role")** | No pinned footer anywhere in `overOpen`. The panel is ONE scroll column (`padding:30px 28px;overflow-y:auto`) and the primary sits inline immediately after the last content block, full-bleed: `display:block;text-align:center;background:#146B33;border-radius:12px;padding:12px` (isPerson `a1L`), or `flex:1` in a row (isStep, isBlock, isAdd). Whitespace, when it exists, falls BELOW the button — the control never floats away from what it commits. | `SettingsDrawer` pins a bordered footer bar (`borderTop`, `padding:16px 26px 22px`) and the person drawer fills it with `<span style={{flex:1}}/>` + a compact right-aligned `Save the role` pill. Last content (the remove link) ends at y≈574; the button sits at y≈836–876 — roughly 250px of empty panel between the role list it saves and the control that saves it. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Prototype convention: every drawer variant closes its content with an inline, full-width primary. The pinned bar is kit-wide (visible on the PAIRED build-drawer-addsender frame too, with "1 OF 3" beside it), so the fix belongs in `bold-settings-kit.tsx` SettingsDrawer — but this surface is where it reads worst, because there are no steps to justify a persistent bar and the body is short.</sub> |
| **Drawer header leading mark** | Every drawer that names a specific entity opens with a leading mark in the header row: 42×42 `border-radius:14` icon tile for the sender (✉/✆, mint or sky tint — see proto-drawer-sender), 44×44 r15 for integrations, and a round avatar for a HUMAN — 62px in `isPerson`, 52px in `isLg`, 32px on `grpRows`. The header is always mark · (eyebrow + title + meta) · ✕. | Header is text-only: `ON YOUR TEAM` eyebrow + `Dr. Ines Duarte` + ✕. No avatar, no initial disc, no icon tile — the one drawer in the set that names a person carries no identity mark at all. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Prototype convention, both branches of it: person-shaped drawers get an avatar, settings-family drawers get the 42px tile. Kit-wide (the build's sender drawer also drops the ✉ tile the prototype and SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS §6 both specify), so add an optional leading slot to SettingsDrawer rather than patching one file.</sub> |
| **Destructive action shape ("Remove from this workspace")** | Destructive drawer actions are full-width tinted pill rows with a leading ✕ glyph: `bg #FBEEEA / border #F0D5CE / color #B0483A, border-radius:13, padding:12px 14px`, sitting as the last item in the action stack (senderDrawer `sActs[2]` — "✕ Remove this sender", visible in proto-drawer-sender). SURFACE_SPEC_SETTINGS §6 states it as "remove/release — destructive in red", i.e. one of the three action rows. | A bare red text link (`fontSize:12.5, fontWeight:700, color:var(--cvb-danger)`) under a hairline divider — no container, no border, no icon, no tint. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Prototype convention. Kit-wide again (the build's sender drawer renders "Pause this sender" the same bare way), so it is an intra-build consistency call as well as a fidelity one.</sub> |
| **Sub-line composition and placement (email + join date)** | One meta line only, and it lives INSIDE the header block beside the title, joined with `·`: `over.meta` = "Prospect · opened twice, no reply" (isPerson), `over.acct` = "Connected · 4 signals matched today" (isInt). No drawer stacks two meta lines. | Two stacked lines, and both are in the scroll body under the header rather than in it: `practice@brightsmile.test` (sans 12.5, muted) then `Joined Aug 31, 2026` (mono 10, faint). They scroll away from the title instead of travelling with it. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Prototype convention, and the build's own team row already composes exactly this way — "practice@brightsmile.test · Owner — everything, …". Fold to one header meta line: `practice@brightsmile.test · joined Aug 31, 2026`.</sub> |
| **Choice rows carry no trailing chip / no marker for the role they already have** | The only radio-choice drawer (`optDrawer` → isOpt) puts a trailing state chip on EVERY row (`r.chip`, `font-size:10px;border-radius:999px`), so the row's current state is legible independent of the ring. The isAdd choice rows carry a trailing ✓ on the selected one. | Four `ChoiceRow`s with the `meta` chip slot left empty on all of them. The filled ring shows only the PENDING pick, so the moment you click Admin nothing on screen still says the member is currently an Owner — and the drawer is what you use to decide. | 🔴 fix<br><sub>Prototype convention plus a real legibility loss; `ChoiceRow` already supports `meta`, and `member.role` is in hand — pass `meta="Current"` (or the "N here" headcount the roles tab already uses) on the row matching `member.role`.</sub> |
| **Closing record line (and ✦ note)** | The `overOpen` template ends entity drawers with the ✦ Ada note + a mono record id (`over.rec`). | **Choice-row composition (ring + two lines, no leading tile, no trailing mark)** | Two distinct choice-row shapes, no third: (a) isOpt — 18px ring + 2px border + 8px dot, ONE line of text (the sub only; the option's name is not rendered), trailing chip, r15 card; (b) isAdd — 28px r10 leading icon tile + title + sub, trailing ✓, r14 card, no ring. | A hybrid: 16px ring + two-line title/sub + no leading tile + no trailing mark, r16 card, mint fill when selected. | 🟢 adopt-build<br><sub>prototype-defect — the prototype has no two-line exclusive-choice row at all, and its isOpt row drops the option's NAME entirely (a role picker that shows only scope sentences would be unusable).
